需要澄清的是，并不是每秒钟都有十亿比特彼此独立、有用的“思想”在你头脑中奔涌。那 10⟦9⟧ 中的很大一部分是生物噪声、重叠的感官输入和冗余的生理信号，把每一个比特都当作一份离散的训练数据是荒谬的。但真正可用的认知状态——意图、犹豫、直觉——就位于高维内部计算与低维行为输出之间那道巨大的鸿沟之中。
Any signal that significantly increases the pool of available data and offers a viable way to improve model alignment and capabilities is worth the bet. Let us run the back of the envelope math to see what a neural corpus actually costs.
任何能显著扩大可用数据池、并提供一种可行方式来提升模型对齐和能力的信号，都值得去押注。让我们做一下粗略估算，看看一个神经语料库实际上要花多少钱。
Current projections indicate that high quality public text on the internet (roughly 300 trillion tokens) will be exhausted by 2028. Recent frontier models already require upward of 15 trillion tokens for a single pre-training run.
目前的预测表明，互联网上的高质量公开文本（约 300 万亿个词元）将在 2028 年耗尽。最近的前沿模型单次预训练就已经需要超过 15 万亿个词元。
To reach that scale using neural data, assuming a conservative extraction rate of 320 token equivalents per second, requires roughly 13 million user hours of recording. As it stands, the field has a massive gap to close.34
要用以每秒 320 个词元当量这一保守提取率的神经数据达到这一规模，大约需要 1,300 万用户小时的记录。就现状而言，这个领域还有巨大的差距需要弥合。⟦34⟧
34. Today, the largest consolidated open EEG corpus, NeuroLM, reaches ~25,000 hours, with efforts targeting 60,000+; however, single coherent datasets remain under 10,000 hours. Meta’s recent TRIBE v2 fMRI corpus contains roughly 500 to 1,100 hours. We are currently two to four orders of magnitude short of that 13-million-hour threshold.
34. 如今，最大的整合开放 EEG 语料库 NeuroLM 达到约 25,000 小时，相关努力的目标是 60,000 小时以上；然而，单一连贯的数据集仍不足 10,000 小时。Meta 最近的 TRIBE v2 fMRI 语料库大约包含 500 到 1,100 小时。我们目前距离那 1,300 万小时的门槛还差两到四个数量级。
If we assume a steep cost of 300 dollars per hour to collect this data, building an illustrative ten million user hour corpus costs exactly 3.0 billion dollars.
如果我们假设采集这些数据的成本高达每小时 300 美元，那么构建一个示意性的一千万用户小时语料库，恰好需要 30 亿美元。
Compare that to current hardware capital expenditures. A single 100,000 GPU cluster costs up to 11 billion dollars. Frontier labs are currently mapping out data centers costing hundreds of billions.35
把这与当前的硬件资本支出相比较。一个拥有 10 万块 GPU 的单一集群造价高达 110 亿美元。前沿实验室目前正在规划造价数千亿美元的数据中心。⟦35⟧
35. OpenAI’s Stargate commitment is reportedly $500B over four years, and the Anthropic-Google TPU deal sits around $200B.
35. 据报道，OpenAI 的“星际之门”（Stargate）承诺为四年 5,000 亿美元，Anthropic 与谷歌的 TPU 交易约 2,000 亿美元。
Against those scales, a 3.0 billion dollar allocation is literally a rounding error, a fraction of a single compute cluster. Ultimately, the frontier labs and the growing robotic industry, driven by an infinite appetite for data, will inevitably become the primary financial engine scaling non-invasive hardware.
以这些规模来看，30 亿美元的拨款简直就是一个四舍五入的误差，只相当于单个计算集群的一小部分。归根结底，在对数据无穷尽的渴求驱动下，前沿实验室和不断壮大的机器人产业，将不可避免地成为规模化非侵入式硬件的主要资金引擎。
The Double Flywheel Effect
双飞轮效应
Normally, in the hardware industry, you need a fully shipped, widely adopted consumer product before you can justify collecting data at scale. Revenue funds data collection, and that data informs the next iteration of the product.
通常，在硬件行业中，你需要一款已经全面推出、被广泛采用的消费级产品，才能证明大规模采集数据是合理的。收入为数据采集提供资金，而这些数据又为产品的下一次迭代提供信息。
BCI already has its own version of this internal loop, described earlier in this manifesto (see The Capital Flywheel): better hardware creates better data, better data trains better decoders, better decoders make the product more useful, and a more useful product gets deployed more widely, generating more data and bringing more capital to improve the hardware.
BCI 已经有了自己版本的这种内部循环，本宣言前面已有描述（见“资本飞轮”）：更好的硬件产生更好的数据，更好的数据训练出更好的解码器，更好的解码器使产品更有用，更有用的产品部署得更广，从而产生更多数据，并带来更多资本来改进硬件。
But neural data adds a massive second feedback loop on top of it.
但神经数据在此之上又叠加了一个巨大的第二反馈回路。
The moment a multi-modal neural corpus gets large and dense enough that pre-training on it actually moves the needle on reasoning or alignment metrics, the frontier AI labs will open their checkbooks. They will buy it at scale the exact same way they bought code corpora, synthetic reasoning traces, and high-fidelity vision-language pairs.
一旦一个多模态神经语料库变得足够大、足够稠密，以至于在其上预训练真正能推动推理或对齐指标，前沿 AI 实验室就会打开支票簿。它们会像购买代码语料库、合成推理轨迹和高保真视觉—语言配对数据一样，大规模地购买它。
Once a frontier lab writes that first eight-figure check for a neural corpus, it will shift the economics of the BCI industry fundamentally.
一旦一家前沿实验室为神经语料库开出第一张八位数的支票，它将从根本上改变 BCI 产业的经济逻辑。
If frontier AI labs value neural datasets directly as an AI training asset, the capital can arrive before the consumer product is even fully baked. The field is no longer waiting solely for consumers to buy enough headsets to fund the next hardware generation, it also has a second buyer with much deeper pockets. A dataset that is prohibitively expensive for a scrappy consumer BCI startup to collect is a rounding error for a frontier AI lab currently spending hundreds of billions on compute cluster build- outs. Redirecting even a fraction of the staggering excess capital in the AI sector into BCI completely shifts the trajectory of the field.
如果前沿 AI 实验室将神经数据集直接作为 AI 训练资产来估值，那么资本可以在消费级产品甚至尚未完全成熟之前就到位。这个领域不再只是等待消费者购买足够多的头戴设备来资助下一代硬件，它还有一个口袋深得多的第二买家。对一家资金紧张的消费级 BCI 初创公司而言高得令人望而却步的数据集采集成本，对一家目前在计算集群建设上花费数千亿美元的前沿 AI 实验室来说，只是一个四舍五入的误差。哪怕只把 AI 行业中惊人过剩资本的一小部分重新导向 BCI，也会彻底改变该领域的轨迹。
Right now, it is unclear which side of the market will hit critical mass first: the consumer pull for non-invasive BCI hardware, or the robotics and frontier labs’ pull for the resulting datasets. In practice, they will likely scale in lockstep, around the same years.
眼下，尚不清楚市场的哪一侧会先达到临界规模：消费者对非侵入式 BCI 硬件的拉力，还是机器人和前沿实验室对其所产生数据集的拉力。在实践中，它们很可能同步规模化，发生在大致相同的年份。
This creates a double flywheel effect, a new data modality with two buyers.
这就产生了双飞轮效应：一种拥有两个买家的新数据模态。
The BCI-AI flywheel is simple: AI capital subsidizes better BCI hardware, better hardware reaches more users, wider deployment produces larger and higher-fidelity neural datasets, and those datasets become useful to AI labs and robotics companies. If the data improves frontier models or robots even slightly, the labs have a reason to reinvest in the BCI ecosystem to secure the next generation of neural data. Better BCI will produce better LLMs, which in turn will fund better BCI.
BCI-AI 飞轮很简单：AI 资本补贴更好的 BCI 硬件，更好的硬件触达更多用户，更广泛的部署产生更大规模、更高保真的神经数据集，而这些数据集对 AI 实验室和机器人公司变得有用。如果这些数据哪怕只是略微改进了前沿模型或机器人，实验室就有理由再投资于 BCI 生态系统，以确保下一代神经数据。更好的 BCI 将产生更好的大语言模型，后者反过来又将为更好的 BCI 提供资金。
We are already seeing the tectonic plates shift. In January 2026, OpenAI led a $252M investment into Merge Labs; Neuralink has an obvious structural proximity to SpaceX / xAI’s Grok; Meta’s FAIR36 division is aggressively scaling its brain foundation models, evidenced by their recent TRIBE v2 publication; and NVIDIA heavily highlighted its collaboration with Synchron at GTC.
我们已经看到地质板块在移动。2026 年 1 月，OpenAI 领投了对 Merge Labs 的 2.52 亿美元投资；Neuralink 与 SpaceX / xAI 的 Grok 有着显而易见的结构性近缘关系；Meta 的 FAIR⟦36⟧ 部门正在积极规模化其大脑基础模型，其最近发表的 TRIBE v2 即为明证；英伟达（NVIDIA）也在 GTC 大会上大篇幅强调了与 Synchron 的合作。
The core of the AI ecosystem is slowly leaning into BCI, even though most of the AI and BCI fields have not yet realized it. In hindsight, the founders and investors positioned on the supply side of this new cycle are going to look very lucky.
AI 生态系统的核心正在慢慢向 BCI 倾斜，尽管 AI 和 BCI 领域的大多数人尚未意识到这一点。事后看来，站在这一新周期供给侧的创始人和投资者会显得非常幸运。
36. FAIR, Meta’s Fundamental AI Research lab, also doubles as a neuroscience lab.
36. FAIR 是 Meta 的基础人工智能研究实验室，同时也兼作神经科学实验室。
Timelines
时间线
If a neural corpus is so fundamentally valuable, why has it not been incorporated already? And why is it not mentioned in the GPT-4 technical report?
如果神经语料库具有如此根本的价值，为什么它尚未被纳入使用？为什么它没有出现在 GPT-4 技术报告中？
The answer comes down to four systemic bottlenecks.
答案归结为四个系统性瓶颈。
First, the complexity threshold. The internet is messy, but raw brain data is on an entirely different level of chaos. A language model from 2016 trying to parse raw EEG data is like a 1980s pocket calculator trying to render a 3D movie. Below a certain scale of compute, adding neural data to the training mix would have just choked the models and hindered progress. It is only in the last 18 to 24 months that frontier models crossed the computational threshold required to actually extract clean, latent signal from the noisy mess of biological brainwaves. The machine finally got smart enough to read the data.
第一，复杂性门槛。互联网固然杂乱，但原始脑数据的混乱程度完全是另一个量级。一个 2016 年的语言模型去解析原始 EEG 数据，就像一台 1980 年代的袖珍计算器去渲染一部 3D 电影。在算力低于某一规模时，把神经数据加入训练混合只会堵塞模型、阻碍进步。直到过去 18 到 24 个月，前沿模型才跨过了从杂乱的生物脑电波中真正提取干净潜在信号所需的计算门槛。机器终于变得足够聪明，能够读取这些数据了。
Second, the hardware bottleneck. Until very recently, brain data simply did not exist at scale. You cannot train a frontier AI on fMRI scans of 15 undergraduate psychology students staring at screens in a hospital basement. You need tens of thousands of hours of continuous, multimodal, naturalistic data. As we covered earlier in this manifesto, consumer-grade BCI is only just now crossing the wearability and form-factor requirements to collect that kind of volume. The data was ignored because the data was scarce.
第二，硬件瓶颈。直到最近，脑数据根本没有以规模化的方式存在。你不能用 15 名本科心理学学生在医院地下室盯着屏幕的 fMRI 扫描来训练一个前沿 AI。你需要数万小时连续、多模态、自然情境下的数据。正如本宣言前面所述，消费级 BCI 直到现在才刚刚跨过采集这种体量所需的可穿戴性和形态要求。数据之所以被忽视，是因为数据太稀缺。
Third, the data wall. We are literally running out of internet. Current projections show that frontier AI training runs will mathematically exhaust the web’s supply of high-quality text between 2027 and 2029. Same story for audio, video, and the rest. As Ilya Sutskever bluntly stated at his NeurIPS keynote in late 2024: “Pre-training as we know it will end. We have achieved peak data. Data is the fossil fuel of AI”. The approaching data wall has forced labs into a hunt for new, unmined reservoirs of signal. While synthetic data and reinforcement learning are heavily used to stretch the timeline, they have hard limits; they are ultimately closed loops that depend on the quality of their initial environments, and that environment is shaped in large part by the data used to build it. Neural data is the next massive, untapped data reservoir.
第三，数据之墙。我们真的正在把互联网用完。目前的预测显示，前沿 AI 训练将在 2027 至 2029 年之间在数学上耗尽网络上高质量文本的供给。音频、视频等其他模态也是同样的故事。正如伊利亚·苏茨克维（Ilya Sutskever）在 2024 年底 NeurIPS 主题演讲中直言的：“我们所熟知的预训练将会终结。我们已经达到了数据峰值。数据是 AI 的化石燃料。”日益临近的数据之墙迫使实验室去寻找尚未开采的新信号储层。尽管合成数据和强化学习被大量用来拉长时间线，但它们有硬性限度；它们归根结底是闭环，依赖于其初始环境的质量，而那个环境在很大程度上是由构建它所用的数据塑造的。神经数据就是下一个庞大的、尚未开发的数据储层。
Fourth, the alignment pivot. For the past decade, AI labs cared primarily about one dimension: capability. The goal was just to make the models smarter, but as AI begins to carry real economic and societal load, the meta-trend has quietly shifted toward alignment: making models trustworthy, steerable, and human-compatible37. You do not just want a hyper-intelligent alien; you want a deeply human-shaped intellect. It is a trend accelerated by increased scrutiny from governments and the public. And to build human-shaped models, labs are increasingly hungry for natively human-shaped data.
第四，对齐转向。过去十年，AI 实验室主要关心一个维度：能力。目标只是让模型更聪明；但随着 AI 开始承担真正的经济和社会负荷，元趋势已悄然转向对齐：让模型变得可信、可引导、与人类相容⟦37⟧。你要的不只是一个超级聪明的外星人，你要的是一个深刻地按人类形态塑造的智力。这一趋势因政府和公众日益增加的审视而加速。而要构建按人类形态塑造的模型，实验室越来越渴求原生具有人类形态的数据。
37. The recent events make that transition even more apparent.
37. 最近发生的事件使这一转变更加明显。
So, when does the dam break?
那么，大坝何时会决口？
My baseline expectation is that by sometime in 2028, at least one of the top three frontier AI labs will publicly announce a major neural-data partnership explicitly framed as a new training modality. You will see a “Neural Corpus” cited in a flagship model card, accompanied by an eight-figure check to acquire the dataset. This will be a turning point in the BCI story.
我的基线预期是：到 2028 年的某个时候，前三家前沿 AI 实验室中至少会有一家公开宣布一项重大的神经数据合作，并明确将其定位为一种新的训练模态。你会在旗舰模型的模型卡中看到“神经语料库”（Neural Corpus）被引用，并伴随着一张用于收购该数据集的八位数支票。这将是 BCI 故事中的一个转折点。
Pre-training, however, is only the first surface of the AI-BCI collision. We now turn to the second surface: interfacing. BCI is about to fundamentally reshape data collection, post-training, inference, and ultimately the way humans communicate with AI.
然而，预训练只是 AI-BCI 碰撞的第一个层面。我们现在转向第二个层面：交互界面。BCI 即将从根本上重塑数据采集、训练后阶段、推理，以及最终人类与 AI 交流的方式。
18 The New Interface
18 新的界面
“The constraint is input/output. We are I/O bound … particularly output … Your output level is so low, particularly on a phone, your two thumbs sort of tapping away. This is ridiculously slow.” — Elon Musk, 2016
“约束在于输入/输出。我们受限于 I/O……尤其是输出……你的输出水平太低了，尤其是在手机上，就靠两个拇指点来点去。这慢得荒唐。”——埃隆·马斯克（Elon Musk），2016 年
We now move beyond pre-training to examine BCI’s role across the rest of the foundation-model lifecycle, across data annotation, post-training, and inference.
我们现在越过预训练，考察 BCI 在基础模型生命周期其余部分中的作用，涵盖数据标注、训练后阶段和推理。
Right now, foundation models can read and process information at gigabytes per second, and text to speech engines can fire back answers instantly. Yet human output, the way we actually prompt the machine, remains stuck in the mud. Prompt engineering, copy pasting context, repeatedly re-explaining yourself: these are all symptoms of a more profound friction.
眼下，基础模型能以每秒数 GB 的速度读取和处理信息，语音合成引擎也能瞬间给出回答。然而人类的输出——我们实际向机器发出提示的方式——仍然陷在泥沼里。提示工程、复制粘贴上下文、反复重新解释自己：这些都是一种更深层摩擦的症状。
We have built a hyper-intelligent, multimodal oracle, but our only way to talk to it is by using our fingers to repeatedly slam plastic squares on a desk at an embarrassing 40 words per minute, or by using vocal cords designed thousands of years ago to grunt across a savanna.
我们建造了一个超智能、多模态的“神谕”，但与它交谈的唯一方式，却是用手指以令人尴尬的每分钟 40 词的速度反复敲击桌上的塑料方块，或者用数千年前为了在稀树草原上发出咕哝而演化出来的声带。
The models may be superhuman, but the human still has to squeeze intent through a straw
模型或许已经超人类，但人类仍须把意图从一根吸管里挤出去。
Sidenote · 旁注
Ironically, computers have their own famous version of this problem: the Von Neumann bottleneck38, where processing power is constrained by the bandwidth between memory and the CPU.
具有讽刺意味的是，计算机也有这个著名问题的自己版本：冯·诺依曼瓶颈（Von Neumann bottleneck）⟦38⟧，即处理能力受限于内存与中央处理器（CPU）之间的带宽。
The fundamental problem is that human intentions are high dimensional, whereas nearly all interfaces are one dimensional. When you prompt a model, you are not actually transmitting your intent. You are writing a lossy postcard from your internal world.
根本问题在于，人类的意图是高维的，而几乎所有界面都是一维的。当你向一个模型发出提示时，你并没有真正传输你的意图。你只是从自己的内部世界寄出一张有损的明信片。
But this postcard misses most of the texture. It strips away the ambiguities, the vague goals, the unspoken preferences, the implicit constraints, the taste, the emotions, and the half-formed corrections. Today, the model often fails not because it lacks intelligence, but because the interface feeds it a degraded, compressed signal.
但这张明信片遗漏了大部分质地。它剥离了含糊之处、模糊的目标、未说出口的偏好、隐含的约束、品味、情绪，以及半成形的修正。今天，模型之所以常常失败，往往不是因为它缺乏智能，而是因为界面喂给它的是一个被降质、被压缩的信号。
38. In classic computer architecture the processor and memory sit on opposite sides of a single shared communication bus, so the CPU can only work as fast as that link can shuttle data and instructions back and forth. Compute can be arbitrarily fast and still stall, starved by the bus feeding it.
38. 在经典计算机体系结构中，处理器和内存分处同一条共享通信总线的两端，因此 CPU 只能以这条链路来回传送数据和指令的速度工作。算力可以任意快，却仍会因总线供养不足而停滞。
The opportunity for BCI, then, extends far beyond pre-training. Neural interfaces could widen the communication channel wherever human judgment enters the AI lifecycle, from data annotation to post-training and inference. We will examine each in turn.
因此，BCI 的机会远远超出预训练。凡是人类判断进入 AI 生命周期的地方——从数据标注到训练后阶段和推理——神经接口都可以拓宽通信通道。我们将依次考察每一处。
BCI-assisted Annotation
BCI 辅助标注
The first application is data annotation. Any dataset requiring human classification, comparison, or evaluation could benefit from faster, higher-fidelity labels.
第一个应用是数据标注。任何需要人类进行分类、比较或评估的数据集，都可以从更快、更高保真的标签中受益。
Consider a simple task: identifying images that contain a dog. At one label every five seconds, someone using a mouse could process roughly 720 images per hour. With a sufficiently accurate neural interface, that person could instead watch a rapid stream of images while the system detects recognition, confidence, uncertainty, and error signals in real time; operating an order of magnitude faster.
设想一个简单任务：识别包含狗的图像。以每五秒一个标签的速度，用鼠标的人每小时大约能处理 720 张图像。若有足够准确的神经接口，此人可以改为观看快速播放的图像流，由系统实时检测识别、置信度、不确定性和错误信号；速度可提高一个数量级。
Annotation would shift from a sequence of deliberate clicks to a continuous stream of perceptual judgments. The gain would not only be speed, each example would carry richer metadata about confidence, ambiguity, cognitive effort etc...
标注将从一连串刻意的点击，转变为连续的感知判断流。收益不仅是速度，每一个样本还会携带关于置信度、歧义、认知努力等更丰富的元数据。
This approach applies to a wide range of annotation tasks. BCI could turn human perception and cognition into a faster and denser labelling channel.
这种方法适用于广泛的标注任务。BCI 可以把人类的感知和认知变成一条更快、更稠密的标注通道。
BCI-RLHF
BCI-RLHF
The same bandwidth bottleneck appears during model post-training, particularly in RLHF. RLHF attempts to align models to human intent by having people rank, score, and correct model outputs, then feeding that judgment back into training. The goal being to make the AI less alien and more aligned to human preferences. Yet here too, both the speed and fidelity with which we can currently communicate human intent to AI are severely limited.
同样的带宽瓶颈也出现在模型的训练后阶段，尤其是在 RLHF 中。RLHF 试图通过让人排列、打分和纠正模型输出，再把这种判断反馈到训练中，从而使模型与人类意图对齐。其目标是让 AI 不那么“异类”，更贴近人类偏好。然而在这里，我们目前向 AI 传达人类意图的速度和保真度同样受到严重限制。
Today, RLHF requires armies of humans manually reading outputs and clicking buttons. It is slow, expensive, and lossy.
今天，RLHF 需要大批人类手动阅读输出并点击按钮。它缓慢、昂贵，而且有损。
Non-invasive BCI could bypass the button click entirely39. The system reads the error-related potential40, cognitive drag, or subconscious aesthetic preference as the evaluator reads. Instead of a binary “thumbs down”, the model receives a rich, continuous vector, the cognitive equivalent of: “The user detected an error in paragraph two, felt a spike of semantic resonance reading option A, experienced cognitive drag when the model overclaimed and their internal preference locked in three full seconds before they actually clicked the button”. It gives the post-training alignment phase a significantly faster and radically more accurate supervisory signal.
非侵入式 BCI 可以完全绕过按钮点击⟦39⟧。系统在评估者阅读的同时，读取错误相关电位⟦40⟧、认知阻力或潜意识的审美偏好。模型收到的不再是一个二元的“点踩”，而是一个丰富、连续的向量，其认知等价物是：“用户在第二段检测到一个错误，阅读选项 A 时感到一阵语义共鸣，当模型过度声称时体验到认知阻力，而且其内部偏好在实际点击按钮之前整整三秒就已经锁定。”它为训练后对齐阶段提供了一个显著更快、准确得多的监督信号。
39. Back of the envelope calculation: An RLHF preference label carries roughly one bit of information: a thumbs-up or an A-versus-B choice. A frontier RLHF cycle may use 10⁵ to 10⁶ such labels, for about 10⁶ bits of compressed judgment in total. By comparison, one second of consumer EEG across 32 channels at 256 Hz and 16-bit resolution contains roughly 1.3 × 10⁵ raw bits. Even if only 0.1% of that signal reflects useful cognitive variables, that still yields around 10² effective bits per second, versus roughly one bit per RLHF click. Neural feedback could therefore be about two orders of magnitude denser per second of cognitive response. At that density, a 10,000-hour dataset would contain roughly 5 × 10⁹ bits of supervision.
39. 粗略估算：一个 RLHF 偏好标签大约携带 1 比特信息：一个点赞，或一个 A 对 B 的选择。一个前沿 RLHF 周期可能使用 10⁵ 到 10⁶ 个这样的标签，总共约 10⁶ 比特的压缩判断。相比之下，1 秒消费级 EEG（32 通道、256 Hz、16 位分辨率）大约包含 1.3 × 10⁵ 个原始比特。即便其中只有 0.1% 反映有用的认知变量，那仍然相当于每秒约 10² 个有效比特，而每次 RLHF 点击大约只有 1 比特。因此，神经反馈在每秒认知反应上的密度可能高出约两个数量级。以这种密度，一个 10,000 小时的数据集将包含大约 5 × 10⁹ 比特的监督信号。
40. An involuntary electrical signature the brain produces within roughly 50-250 ms of registering an error or an outcome that violates what it expected, readable non-invasively.
40. 一种不由自主的电信号特征，大脑在登记到一个错误或一个违背其预期的结果后大约 50–250 毫秒内产生，可以非侵入式读取。
Brain signals can capture confusion, hesitation, error recognition, preference, attention, fatigue, cognitive load, or discomfort. This creates faster, denser, more honest reward signals. Instead of “rate answer A vs B”, the model gets a continuous stream of human reaction while the AI actions are being evaluated.
脑信号可以捕捉困惑、犹豫、错误识别、偏好、注意力、疲劳、认知负荷或不适。这创造出更快、更稠密、更诚实的奖励信号。模型得到的不再是“给答案 A 和 B 打分”，而是在评估 AI 行为时人类反应的连续数据流。
RLHF becomes continuous instead of episodic. Creating a much tighter human-AI loop.
RLHF 从片段式变为连续式，从而形成一个紧密得多的人机回路。
To make it less abstract, let’s look at image generation. It is notoriously hard to teach a cluster of GPUs to have “taste” using a keyboard. How do you type out exactly why an image feels beautiful, or why a certain minimalist design just works?
为了不那么抽象，让我们看看图像生成。用键盘去教一堆 GPU 拥有“品味”，向来极为困难。你如何精确地打出为什么一幅图像让人觉得美，或者为什么某种极简设计就是恰到好处？
Today, RLHF reduces this to a 1 to 10 rating or an A/B choice. Useful, but tragically compressed. With a non-invasive BCI, you don’t click anything, you can just look and the AI directly absorbs your raw neurological reaction, providing the model with a much richer feedback signal.
今天，RLHF 把这压缩成 1 到 10 的评分或 A/B 选择。有用，但压缩得令人惋惜。有了非侵入式 BCI，你什么都不用点，只需观看，AI 就会直接吸收你原始的神经反应，为模型提供丰富得多的反馈信号。
Sidenote · 旁注
Of course there are also problems that come with this approach. If you optimize a model to minimize the reader’s cognitive friction and maximize neural “pleasure”41, you get sycophancy and easy-but-wrong answers. Optimizing System-1 neural reward ≠ optimizing what the user would endorse on reflection. A denser signal is not strictly better. However, this is a tractable problem that is not unique to this approach.
当然，这种方法也带来一些问题。如果你优化一个模型去最小化读者的认知摩擦、最大化神经“愉悦”⟦41⟧，你就会得到谄媚和轻松但错误的答案。优化系统 1（System-1）的神经奖励，并不等于优化用户在反思后会认可的东西。更稠密的信号并不严格地更好。不过，这是一个可以处理的问题，而且并非这种方法所独有。
Some readers will rightly point out that labs are increasingly pivoting toward AI feedback (RLAIF)42 and synthetic data. True, but synthetic environments are ultimately a closed loop, they are fantastic for amplifying what we already know, like running endless physics simulations for a robot. But they hit a brick wall when dealing with the messy, unmapped territory of human cognition. You can synthesize a physics engine, but you cannot easily synthetically generate taste, moral friction, or implicit preference. For the deep, subjective training and alignment of an AI, there is no easy substitute for biological ground truth.
有些读者会正确地指出，实验室正日益转向 AI 反馈（RLAIF）⟦42⟧和合成数据。没错，但合成环境归根结底是一个闭环：它们非常擅长放大我们已经知道的东西，比如为机器人运行无穷无尽的物理模拟。但在面对人类认知那片杂乱、尚未测绘的领地时，它们会撞上砖墙。你可以合成一个物理引擎，但你无法轻易地合成出品味、道德摩擦或隐性偏好。对于 AI 深层的、主观的训练与对齐而言，生物层面的“地面实况”（ground truth）没有简单的替代品。
Brain Controlled AI
脑控 AI
Going further into the AI lifecycle, BCI could fundamentally redefine how people interact with AI. Since the launch of ChatGPT, it has become increasingly clear that the iPhone, the laptop, and today’s operating systems were not built for this new species of software. They force intelligence into old and increasingly misshaped containers: apps, menus, keyboards, taps, and tiny text boxes.
再往 AI 生命周期深处看，BCI 可以从根本上重新定义人们与 AI 的交互方式。自 ChatGPT 推出以来，越来越清楚的是：iPhone、笔记本电脑和今天的操作系统，并不是为这一新物种的软件而建造的。它们把智能硬塞进越来越不合身的旧容器里：应用、菜单、键盘、轻点，以及小小的文本框。
As AI becomes more capable, the interface will have to change. It will become more fluid, more contextual, more intent-driven. Less “open app, type command, wait” and more “think goal, steer intelligence”. Among the leading candidates for that shift is BCI.
随着 AI 能力增强，界面必须改变。它将变得更流畅、更有情境、更由意图驱动。少一些“打开应用、输入命令、等待”，多一些“想到目标、引导智能”。这一转变的主要候选方案之一，就是 BCI。
41. Researchers can already optimize generated images to activate specific brain regions.
41. 研究人员已经能够优化生成的图像，以激活特定的脑区。
42. RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) is similar to RLHF, except the preference judgments come from another AI model rather than a human labeler; it is used to cut the cost and latency of human annotation.
42. RLAIF（来自 AI 反馈的强化学习，Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback）类似于 RLHF，只是偏好判断来自另一个 AI 模型而非人类标注者；它被用来降低人类标注的成本和延迟。
A brain-computer interface is the most direct way to interact with an intelligence acting on your behalf. You form an intention, it understands what you mean, and it acts.
脑机接口是与一个代表你行动的智能体交互的最直接方式。你形成一个意图，它理解你的意思，然后采取行动。
Right now, if you want an AI to perform a complex task, you have to spend 20 minutes typing out a massive prompt to artificially context-bound its thinking, and even then, it is often under- contextualized. With continuous BCI, the AI natively inherits your ambient cognitive context (what you are looking at, your current frustration level, your unstated goals) before you even open your mouth. The prompting is happening continuously in the background.
眼下，如果你想让 AI 完成一项复杂任务，你必须花 20 分钟打出一段冗长的提示，人为地为其思考划定上下文，即便如此，上下文也常常不足。有了连续的 BCI，AI 在你开口之前就原生继承了你周围的认知上下文（你在看什么、当前的沮丧程度、未言明的目标）。提示一直在后台持续发生。
If BCI continues on the current trajectory, this shift is inevitable.
如果 BCI 沿着当前轨迹继续前进，这种转变是不可避免的。
This is the natural historical progression of technology: every new wave reduces the distance between intent and outcome. The ultimate shape of software and AI will be thought-to-pixel and thought-to- robotic-actuation, with nothing between intent and outcome but the raw model weights. The labs will produce models whose inputs and outputs are increasingly close to the bare hardware of both humans and machines, with every generation stripping away another layer of biological and software processing. As this gap closes and thought-to-X grows, brain data is going to become as valuable to frontier labs as text was in the prompt-to-text era.
这是技术自然的历史进程：每一波新浪潮都缩短意图与结果之间的距离。软件和 AI 的终极形态将是“思想到像素”和“思想到机器人执行”，在意图与结果之间，除了模型的原始权重之外别无他物。实验室将产出输入和输出越来越接近人与机器裸硬件的模型，每一代都再剥去一层生物与软件处理。随着这一差距缩小、“思想到 X”不断扩展，脑数据对前沿实验室的价值，将变得如同“提示到文本”时代的文本一样宝贵。
But we are not going to magically jump from poking screens to telepathically steering robots overnight, the transition will happen in progressive steps. In part because brain decoders still need a few years to mature, but mostly because human culture has strict speed limits for paradigm shifts. Society has to be gradually acclimated to this level of symbiosis.
但我们不会一夜之间魔法般地从戳屏幕跳到用心灵感应驾驭机器人，这一过渡将分阶段发生。部分原因是脑解码器还需要几年才能成熟，但更主要的是，人类文化对范式转变有着严格的速度限制。社会必须逐渐适应这种程度的共生。
The path to full neural control will probably happen in layers. At first, BCI will not read your thoughts. It will read the useful edge signals around them: attention, confusion, fatigue, frustration, and the moment you notice something is wrong; allowing the AI to adapt in real time to what you are noticing, missing, or struggling with. From there, the interface becomes more active: silent selection, approval, rejection, simple commands, and eventually subvocal dictation. And ultimately, it will combine into something far more powerful: an omnipresent AI cognitive layer, or exocortex, functioning as a direct extension of the mind.
通往完全神经控制的路径很可能分层发生。起初，BCI 不会读取你的思想。它会读取思想周围那些有用的边缘信号：注意力、困惑、疲劳、沮丧，以及你注意到出错的那一刻；从而使 AI 能够实时适应你正在注意、遗漏或挣扎的内容。从那里开始，界面变得更主动：无声选择、批准、拒绝、简单指令，最终是默读口授。最终，它将组合成某种强大得多的东西：一个无处不在的 AI 认知层，即外皮层，作为心智的直接延伸而运作。
This neural control will also inevitably cross into physical reality. As artificial intelligence escapes the screen to animate drones, factory floors, and military hardware, the brain-computer interface will act as the invisible tether connecting human intent to physical execution, granting us the ability to telepathically direct embodied agents and robotics.
这种神经控制也必将延伸到物理现实。随着人工智能走出屏幕，为无人机、工厂车间和军事硬件注入活力，脑机接口将充当把人类意图连接到物理执行的无形绳索，使我们能够以心灵感应的方式指挥具身智能体和机器人。
We are converging on a world, quite literally, shaped by our minds. By the early 2030s, it will be possible to control a computer, command a drone or interact with home robots simply through the power of your thoughts.
我们正在汇聚于一个从字面意义上由我们的心智所塑造的世界。到 2030 年代初期，人们将能够仅凭思想的力量控制电脑、指挥无人机或与家用机器人交互。
Life in 2031
2031 年的生活
At 7:15 AM, Maya is in the kitchen. She has her two-year-old daughter balanced on her hip while she plates her breakfast. Floating two feet in front of her is a 12-page licensing agreement. She is not tapping a screen or shouting commands, she is just reading through her lightweight AR glasses.
早上 7:15，玛雅（Maya）在厨房里。她一边把两岁的女儿揽在胯边，一边摆上早餐。在她前方两英尺处悬浮着一份 12 页的许可协议。她没有点屏幕，也没有喊出指令，只是透过轻便的增强现实（AR）眼镜阅读。
The text slides upward, perfectly matching her natural reading speed. She hits a messy indemnification clause in paragraph four and her brain registers a tiny flinch of frustration. Before Maya even has to articulate why she dislikes the clause, her neural interface logs the friction. The floating text seamlessly shifts: the AI highlights the offending line and instantly surfaces a cleaner alternative that protects her IP.
文字向上滑动，完美匹配她自然的阅读速度。她在第四段碰到一条乱七八糟的赔偿条款，大脑登记到一丝细微的沮丧。玛雅甚至还没来得及说出她为什么不喜欢这条——接口已经记下了这种摩擦。悬浮的文字无缝变化：AI 高亮出那行有问题的条款，并立刻给出一个更能保护她知识产权的更干净替代方案。
Behind her, the house robot is putting away the morning dishes. It places her daughter’s favorite bowl in the top cupboard instead of the lower drawer. Maya does not say a word, but as she glances over, her brain fires a split-second spark of disapproval at the mistake. The system catches the silent correction immediately, the robot turns back around, retrieves the bowl, and places it exactly where it belongs.
在她身后，家用机器人正在收拾早上的碗碟。它把女儿最喜欢的碗放进了上层橱柜，而不是下层抽屉。玛雅一言未发，但当她瞥过去时，大脑因这个错误迸发出一瞬不赞同的火花。系统立刻捕捉到这次无声的纠正，机器人转过身，取回那只碗，把它放回该放的地方。
Glancing out the kitchen window at the frost on the driveway, Maya deliberately projects a thought to her assistant: Is the garage locked? Also, it looks freezing out there, can you prep the car for the preschool run?
玛雅望向厨房窗外车道上的霜，有意识地向助手投射一个想法：车库锁了吗？外面看起来很冷，你能把车准备好，好送孩子去幼儿园吗？
She does not open an app or even whisper. A half-second later, a quiet voice speaks directly through her interface: “The garage is locked. I am starting the car now and turning on the heated seats. It will be ready when you walk out.”
她没有打开任何应用，甚至没有低声说话。半秒后，一个轻柔的声音直接通过她的接口响起：“车库已锁好。我正在启动汽车并打开座椅加热。等你走出去时，车就会准备就绪。”
She turns her attention to an urgent work email floating in her peripheral vision. Maya just reads it, allowing her mind to form a raw, unedited reaction: a few structural objections and a firm timeline. As she thinks through her response, a draft reply begins to materialize right next to the original message. The words reshape themselves in real time as her thoughts refine, shifting from a loose skeleton into a perfectly polished professional response.
她把注意力转向在余光中悬浮的一封紧急工作邮件。玛雅只是读着，让脑海中形成一个原始、未经编辑的反应：几点结构性的反对意见，以及一个坚定的时间表。随着她思考回复，一份草稿回复开始在原邮件旁边显现。文字随着她的想法细化而实时重塑，从松散的骨架变成一份打磨完美的专业回复。
Maya mentally drops a silent approve command, and the email sends.
玛雅在心里发出一个无声的批准指令，邮件发出。
The technology is not flashing lights or demanding her attention, it operates as an invisible, high-bandwidth exocortex, an external nervous system. Maya is no longer “using a computer”; she is orchestrating the physical and digital infrastructure at the speed of her own consciousness.
这项技术并不闪烁灯光或争夺她的注意，它作为一层看不见的、高带宽的外皮层运作，像一套外部神经系统。玛雅不再是“在使用电脑”；她正在以自己意识的速度编排物理与数字基础设施。
A slow morning in 2031.
2031 年一个缓慢的早晨。
Yet removing friction from the interface does not solve the deeper problem: whether the intelligence on the other side will act in our interests. As AI becomes more capable and autonomous, communication becomes more than a question of usability. It becomes a question of whether humans can remain meaningfully involved in directing it. This brings us to the third and final surface of the AI-BCI collision: alignment.
然而，消除界面上的摩擦并不能解决更深层的问题：另一端的智能是否会以我们的利益行事。随着 AI 变得更有能力、更自主，沟通不再只是一个可用性的问题。它变成了人类能否有意义地参与引导它的问题。这就把我们带到 AI-BCI 碰撞的第三个、也是最后一个层面：对齐。
19 Why This Matters for AI Alignment
19 为什么这对 AI 对齐至关重要
“we had better be quite sure that the purpose put into the machine is the purpose which we really desire.” — Norbert Wiener, 1960
“我们最好相当确定，放入机器中的目的，就是我们真正想要的目的。”——诺伯特·维纳（Norbert Wiener），1960 年
Most people don’t love to die. And unless you do, you should probably care about alignment.
大多数人并不喜欢死去。除非你喜欢，否则你大概应该关心对齐。
Sidenote · 旁注
For those of us who have never heard of AI alignment, it is the process of making sure that systems more capable than us do what we want, and keep doing it as they get more powerful. It sounds simple, almost boring, but it is one of the most important and complex engineering problems our species ever had to take on. And right now, we have no idea how to solve it; and no idea how to ensure increasingly capable AI remains coupled to human values, oversight, and flourishing.
对于从未听说过 AI 对齐的人来说，它是这样一个过程：确保比我们更有能力的系统做我们想要的事，并且在它们变得更强大时继续这样做。这听起来简单，几乎乏味，但它是我们这个物种曾经面对过的最重要、最复杂的工程问题之一。而眼下，我们不知道如何解决它；也不知道如何确保日益强大的 AI 始终与人类的价值观、监督和繁荣相耦合。
The field’s circulating meme about what we are aligning: a vast, alien intelligence with a thin smiley mask (RLHF) bolted on top. Alignment questions whether the mask is the model, or just a facade.
这个领域流传的迷因，描述的是我们正在对齐的对象：一个庞大的、异己的智能，上面只钉着一层薄薄的笑脸面具（RLHF）。对齐要追问的是：这层面具就是模型本身，还是只是一层表象。
Just like everything under the sun, alignment has become weirdly politicized, partly because of the poor communication around the topic, and partly because of a fundamental lack of understanding of AI from the public. But if any topic should be apolitical, it is certainly alignment. There is no culture war here, just a pretty straightforward problem with no solution in sight. If we lose, we are all equally screwed.
就像天下万事一样，对齐也奇怪地被政治化了，部分是因为围绕这一话题的沟通糟糕，部分是因为公众对 AI 根本缺乏理解。但如果有哪个话题应当是非政治的，那一定是对齐。这里没有文化战争，只有一个相当直白、却看不到解决方案的问题。如果我们失败，所有人都会同样遭殃。
No one knows what happens over the next decade, and you should distrust anyone who pretends they do. Utopia, dystopia, and everything in between are still on the table. We are flying into a New World at full speed, with no manual and no guardrails.
没有人知道未来十年会发生什么，你应当不信任任何假装自己知道的人。乌托邦、反乌托邦，以及介于两者之间的一切，都仍在牌桌上。我们正全速飞入一个新世界，既没有手册，也没有护栏。
The window of time during which humanity can still effectively steer the trajectory of AI, the Phase Lock Window, is closing a bit more every day.
人类仍能有效引导 AI 轨迹的时间窗口——相位锁定窗口——每一天都在缩小一点。
One logical response to this alignment conundrum is restraint: pause deployment, constrain capability until safety catches up. This is a legitimate argument, that a serious coalition of researchers and policymakers continues to argue for, but it fails on game theory. Two frontier labs or nation-states racing for AGI are unlikely to unilaterally slow down. Under current incentives, restraint is a losing equilibrium without a binding coordination mechanism. And while this deadlock is ongoing, alignment research is lagging dangerously behind43.
对这一对齐困局的一种合乎逻辑的回应是克制：暂停部署，在安全赶上之前约束能力。这是一个正当的论点，仍有一批认真的研究者和政策制定者在持续主张，但它在博弈论上站不住脚。两家前沿实验室或两个民族国家在竞逐 AGI 时，不太可能单方面放慢速度。在当前的激励结构下，若没有具有约束力的协调机制，克制是一种会失败的均衡。而在这一僵局持续的同时，对齐研究正在危险地落后。⟦43⟧
43. When thinking about AI alignment, it is tempting to look for a villain in the story: a rival country, a greedy tech company, or some dark room full of bad actors. But the reality is that we are all in the exact same boat, rocked left and right by the sea of uncertainty we are navigating, trying to remain afloat personally and collectively. If this goes wrong, everybody sinks; the only true antagonist is the system of incentives of modern society more than any specific individuals.
43. 在思考 AI 对齐时，人们很容易想在故事里找一个反派：一个敌对国家、一家贪婪的科技公司，或某个坐满坏人的暗室。但现实是，我们都在同一条船上，被正在航行的不确定之海左右摇晃，努力让自己和集体保持浮出水面。如果这件事出了问题，所有人都会沉没；真正的对手与其说是任何特定的个人，不如说是现代社会的激励体系。
And to make things worse, alignment is currently in direct tension with the economic incentives of the labs. Indeed, making a model safer can sometimes make it dumber, slower, or more expensive to train. This “alignment tax” is why labs cut corners. Current RLHF often lobotomizes models, making them refuse benign requests or hide underlying capabilities.
雪上加霜的是，对齐目前与实验室的经济激励直接冲突。事实上，让模型更安全，有时会让它更笨、更慢，或训练成本更高。正是这种“对齐税”（alignment tax）让实验室走捷径。当前的 RLHF 常常给模型做“脑叶切除”，使它们拒绝无害的请求，或隐藏潜在的能力。
Alignment-by-control vs. Alignment-by-integration
控制式对齐与融合式对齐
Ultimately, every approach to alignment falls into one of two families: alignment-by-control and alignment-by-integration. Control treats AI as a separate agent and attempts to steer it from the outside through rules, oversight, interpretability, and governance. While integration takes the opposite route: it couples human intent to the machine directly, so the steering happens from inside the system rather than around it.
归根结底，所有对齐方法都属于两个家族之一：控制式对齐和融合式对齐。控制把 AI 当作一个分离的主体，试图通过规则、监督、可解释性和治理从外部引导它。而融合则走相反的路线：它把人类意图直接耦合到机器上，使引导从系统内部发生，而不是在系统周围发生。
In the short term, control is indispensable, it buys us time while the mechanisms for deeper integration remain immature. But control runs on borrowed time by construction: it works while the intelligence gap is small, and as AI becomes orders of magnitude faster and more capable, external supervision grows increasingly fragile. Eventually, asking humans to control decisions they can no longer follow may resemble asking an ant to supervise a human, a rather precarious position for the ant.
短期内，控制不可或缺，它为我们争取时间，而更深层融合的机制仍未成熟。但控制从建构上就是在透支时间：它在智能差距尚小的时候有效，而随着 AI 变得快出几个数量级、能力强出几个数量级，外部监督会越来越脆弱。最终，要求人类去控制他们已无法跟上的决策，可能就像要求一只蚂蚁去监督一个人类，对蚂蚁而言，这是一个相当危险的位置。
While we are still in the Phase Lock Window, control remains strong enough for humanity to determine the terms of our relationship with AI. But as AI becomes 10x, 100x, or 1000x smarter than us, alignment-by-integration becomes the only viable solution. Unlike control, which has to be rebuilt every time the machine gets smarter, integration does not compete with the machine’s intelligence, it inherits it. Bringing human intent inside the AI cognition loop offers a relationship that can remain meaningful even as AI scales beyond us.
当我们仍处于相位锁定窗口之内时，控制仍然强大到足以让人类决定我们与 AI 关系的条款。但当 AI 变得比我们聪明 10 倍、100 倍或 1,000 倍时，融合式对齐就成为唯一可行的方案。与必须在机器每次变得更聪明时都重新构建的控制不同，融合并不与机器的智能竞争，它继承这种智能。把人类意图带入 AI 认知回路内部，提供了一种即使 AI 规模化到超越我们，依然可以保持意义的关系。
So how can BCI, and neurotechnology more broadly, help with alignment?
那么，BCI 以及更广义的神经技术，如何能帮助对齐？
BCI sits in a category of its own within the alignment field, it is one of the only approaches centered on integration. Interpretability, governance, oversight, or evaluations all focus primarily on controlling the machine. BCI attempts to expand the human reach rather than just restraining AI.
BCI 在对齐领域中自成一类，它是少数以融合为中心的方法之一。可解释性、治理、监督或评估，都主要着眼于控制机器。BCI 试图扩展人类的触及范围，而不仅仅是约束 AI。
On top of the benefit of using brain data for pre-training and post-training to better capture the cognitive dark matter of the mind and make AI intelligence less jagged, BCI can make a meaningful contribution to the future of alignment and humanity through three main alignment levers with increasingly long time horizons. First, better BCI leads to a better empirical map of the human brain as a direct side-effect, which helps inform human-centric AI architecture, training, and evaluation. Second, BCI creates a continuous, high-bandwidth communication channel between AI and humans, offering a more effective way to interact with AI. Third, it lays the foundation for human-AI symbiosis, ensuring humans stay meaningfully in the loop as the machine gets exponentially smarter.
除了利用脑数据进行预训练和训练后阶段、以更好地捕捉心智的认知暗物质并使 AI 智能更少参差不齐之外，BCI 还可以通过三根时间视野逐渐拉长的主要对齐杠杆，为对齐和人类的未来做出有意义的贡献。第一，更好的 BCI 会作为直接的副作用，带来一幅更好的人脑经验地图，从而帮助以人类为中心的 AI 架构、训练和评估。第二，BCI 在 AI 与人类之间创造一条连续的高带宽通信通道，提供一种更有效的与 AI 交互的方式。第三，它为人机共生奠定基础，确保在机器以指数速度变得更聪明时，人类仍然有意义地留在回路之中。
Lever 1: The Blueprint
杠杆 1：蓝图
As non-invasive BCI grows into a large consumer industry and data source for the AI labs, it will draw capital, talent, and attention into neurotechnology. That influx will accelerate our empirical understanding of the human brain. We will finally start to crack and decode the machinery of our own minds. That cognitive blueprint could become a powerful design resource for AI alignment.
随着非侵入式 BCI 成长为一个大型消费产业和 AI 实验室的数据来源，它将把资本、人才和注意力吸入神经技术。这一涌入将加速我们对人脑的经验性理解。我们终于将开始破解和解码自己心智的机制。这份认知蓝图可以成为 AI 对齐的强大设计资源。
We still do not know how values should be represented inside an artificial mind, how competing objectives should be reconciled, or how immediate impulses should relate to reflective judgment. Human cognition is the only working example of an intelligence in which reasoning, emotion, social intuition, and ethical values coexist. Understanding the architecture of the only working general intelligence whose values we are actually trying to preserve seems like a wise place to start.
我们仍然不知道价值观应如何在一个人工心智中被表征，相互竞争的目标应如何被调和，或即时冲动应如何与反思性判断相关联。人类认知是唯一一个把推理、情绪、社会直觉和伦理价值观共存于其中的、已经在运作的智能实例。去理解我们实际上想要保存其价值观的、唯一已经在运作的通用智能的架构，似乎是一个明智的起点。
We already have compelling evidence that across language, vision, space, and time, biological and artificial intelligence are converging on some shared internal representations44. In other words, some AI models organize information in ways that resemble the human mind, structuring concepts such as faces, living things, and roundness along similar dimensions. This does not imply that they compute every single concept exactly as we do, but it does suggest that sufficiently capable predictive systems may independently discover some of the same useful abstractions.
我们已经有令人信服的证据表明，在语言、视觉、空间和时间上，生物智能与人工智能正在向某些共享的内部表征汇聚⟦44⟧。换句话说，一些 AI 模型组织信息的方式类似于人脑，沿着相似的维度来结构面孔、生物、圆度等概念。这并不意味着它们计算每一个概念的方式都与我们完全相同，但它确实表明，足够强大的预测系统可能独立发现一些同样有用的抽象。
Such emerging similarities reinforce the idea that a better understanding of the mind could effectively inform model design, training objectives, and evaluations. Rather than attaching a thin behavioural mask after training, labs could build systems whose internal representations and architecture are better matched to the structure of human values from the beginning.
这种正在显现的相似性强化了这样一个想法：更好地理解心智，可以有效地为模型设计、训练目标和评估提供信息。实验室不必在训练后再贴上一层薄薄的行为面具，而可以从一开始就构建内部表征和架构更匹配人类价值观结构的系统。
This does not mean blindly copying the brain, nor does it guarantee alignment; human cognition is inconsistent, biased, and often destructive. The point is to replace guesswork with an empirical blueprint of the value architecture we are trying to preserve. In this sense, even a partial map would provide valuable guidance for designing more human-like artificial minds.
这并不意味着盲目复制大脑，也不能保证对齐；人类认知是不一致的、有偏见的，而且常常具有破坏性。要点在于，用一幅关于我们试图保存的价值架构的经验蓝图，来取代猜测。就此而言，即便是一幅部分地图，也能为设计更像人类的人工心智提供宝贵指导。
Sidenote · 旁注
To go one step further, my bet is that the brain’s influence on AI will extend beyond alignment. Future generations of models will not just be marginally inspired by the brain, they will become increasingly brain-like in their overall design; simply because the brain, in many ways, has a fundamentally better architecture. They will become more cyclical, with inputs and outputs interacting more continuously and organically with the model, and have more adaptive and flexible architectures. The main reason this has not happened yet is that recurrent systems are harder to train and interpret than predominantly feedforward ones, but I expect this barrier to be overcome soon.
再往前走一步，我的押注是：大脑对 AI 的影响将超出对齐。未来几代模型将不只是略微受到大脑启发，它们将在整体设计上变得越来越像大脑；仅仅因为在许多方面，大脑从根本上拥有更好的架构。它们将变得更循环，输入与输出将更连续、更有机地与模型相互作用，并拥有更适应、更灵活的架构。这件事至今尚未发生的主要原因，是循环系统比以前馈为主的系统更难训练和解释，但我预期这一障碍很快就会被克服。
44. See Xu et al., Revealing emergent human-like conceptual representations from language prediction (PNAS, 2025); Du et al., Human-like object concept representations emerge naturally in multimodal large language models (Nature Machine Intelligence, 2025); Gurnee and Tegmark, Language Models Represent Space and Time (ICLR, 2024); and Bogdan et al., Mapping concept and relational semantic representation in the brain using large language models (Current Biology, 2026).
44. 参见 Xu 等，《从语言预测中揭示涌现的类人概念表征》（PNAS，2025）；Du 等，《类人物体概念表征在多模态大语言模型中自然涌现》（Nature Machine Intelligence，2025）；Gurnee 与 Tegmark，《语言模型表征空间与时间》（ICLR，2024）；以及 Bogdan 等，《利用大语言模型绘制大脑中的概念与关系语义表征》（Current Biology，2026）。
Lever 2: Neural Scalable Oversight
杠杆 2：神经可扩展监督
But architecture is only one part of the equation.
但架构只是方程的一部分。
An intelligent system you cannot communicate with fast enough is a system that drifts away from you. Imagine a colleague who can think a hundred times faster than you, and who only reports back with a one-line text summary every few minutes. You can supervise them, sort of, but only in retrospect. The relationship turns from collaboration into oversight, and oversight at low frequency is exactly the dynamic where misalignment grows.
一个你无法足够快地与之沟通的智能系统，就是一个会逐渐远离你的系统。想象一位思考速度比你快一百倍的同事，每隔几分钟才用一行文字摘要向你汇报。你可以对他们进行某种监督，但只是事后监督。这种关系从协作变成了监管，而低频监管恰恰是错位容易滋长的动态。
Right now, we are trying to align systems that think at machine speed using feedback channels designed for humans filling out forms. The current alignment stack rests on a one-bit RLHF click feeding models that output thousands of tokens per second. That was always going to eventually break. BCI provides a high-bandwidth alternative that actually can scale with AI capability. Neural interfaces can fill this gap in three specific ways.
眼下，我们正试图用为“填表的人”设计的反馈通道，去对齐以机器速度思考的系统。当前的对齐技术栈依赖于一个 1 比特的 RLHF 点击，去喂养每秒输出数千个词元的模型。这终究是要崩溃的。BCI 提供了一种真正能够与 AI 能力同步规模化的高带宽替代方案。神经接口可以从三个具体方面填补这一缺口。
First, as ground truth for value specification. Value specification, the act of mathematically defining what humans actually care about, is impossibly hard.45 These values are fluid, constantly shifting over time and varying wildly across different people. Plus human morality and intent are full of implicit dark matter: contexts, unspoken boundaries, and common-sense elements that we intuitively know but cannot exhaustively write down in a prompt or a reward function. Brain data gives us the biological ground truth of human preference to serve as the most direct reference channel possible. It captures the raw shape of our ethics and values without forcing us to compress them into clumsy text rules, leaving less room for the AI to misunderstand our intent.
第一，作为价值规范的地面实况。价值规范——从数学上定义人类真正在乎什么——难到几乎不可能。⟦45⟧ 这些价值观是流动的，随时间不断变化，在不同人之间差异巨大。加之人类的道德和意图充满隐性的暗物质：情境、未言明的边界，以及我们凭直觉知道、却无法在提示词或奖励函数中穷尽写下的常识要素。脑数据为我们提供人类偏好的生物地面实况，成为尽可能最直接的参考通道。它捕捉我们伦理和价值观的原始形态，而不迫使我们把它们压缩成笨拙的文本规则，从而减少 AI 误解我们意图的空间。
And it is not just theoretical science fiction, the mechanism for using neural data as an alignment signal is past the theoretical stage: in early 2026, Santaniello et al. published a paper operationalizing Reinforcement Learning from Neural Feedback (RLNF) on a 25-participant dataset, successfully decoding agent performance directly from brain activity.
这也不只是理论上的科幻。将神经数据用作对齐信号的机制已经越过了理论阶段：2026 年初，Santaniello 等人发表了一篇论文，在一个 25 名受试者的数据集上操作化了“来自神经反馈的强化学习”（RLNF），成功地直接从脑活动中解码出智能体的表现。
This goes beyond the BCI-RLHF described earlier: the broader goal is to have an effective way to decode the overall structure of human values from neural activity and integrate it deeper into the training and supervision process of AI.
这超越了前面描述的 BCI-RLHF：更广泛的目标是，找到一种有效的方式，从神经活动中解码人类价值观的整体结构，并将其更深入地整合到 AI 的训练与监督过程中。
45. Specification gaming is the most anticipated failure mode of AI alignment. If you use text to tell an AI to “make humans happy”, it might logically conclude the most efficient way to do that is to wire our brains to heroin drips. There are plenty of write-ups on the topic such as DeepMind’s specification gaming overview and Victoria Krakovna’s running list of real-world examples.
45. “规范博弈”（specification gaming）是 AI 对齐最被预期的失败模式。如果你用文字告诉 AI“让人类快乐”，它可能在逻辑上得出最有效的方式是把我们的大脑接到海洛因滴注上。关于这一话题有大量论述，例如 DeepMind 的规范博弈综述，以及维多利亚·克拉科夫娜（Victoria Krakovna）持续更新的真实世界案例清单。
Second, as a decoder for inner alignment. One of the best approaches to catch deceptive models is looking under the hood to understand exactly how artificial neurons wire together, a field known as mechanistic interpretability. Neuroscience is essentially a multi-decade head start on this problem. The math BCI uses to decode human thought translates remarkably well to decoding AI internals. Plus, a brain map gives us sharper benchmarks, if we know exactly what human uncertainty, attention, or moral hesitation looks like structurally, we know exactly what to look for when we scan the internals of the AI.
第二，作为内在对齐的解码器。捕捉欺骗性模型的最佳方法之一，是打开引擎盖，精确理解人工神经元如何连接在一起——这一领域被称为机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）。神经科学本质上已经在这个问题上领先了数十年。BCI 用来解码人类思想的数学，以惊人的方式同样适用于解码 AI 的内部。此外，一幅大脑地图给我们更锐利的基准：如果我们确切知道人类的不确定性、注意力或道德犹豫在结构上是什么样子，我们就确切知道在扫描 AI 内部时该寻找什么。
Some recent findings from Anthropic illustrate this idea. In July 2026, they reported that frontier models spontaneously form a “global workspace”, a centralized state for holding and reusing concepts, which is bypassed by routine computations. It functions much like our own active memory and its discovery was inspired by neurotech. Finding this exact parallel in silicon demonstrates how understanding the biological brain maps directly onto machine interpretability.
Anthropic 的一些近期发现说明了这一想法。2026 年 7 月，他们报告说，前沿模型会自发形成一个“全局工作空间”（global workspace），一个用于保存和复用概念的中心化状态，而日常计算会绕过它。它的功能很像我们自己的主动记忆，其发现也受到神经技术的启发。在硅基中发现这一精确的平行现象，表明对生物大脑的理解如何直接映射到机器的可解释性上。
Third, as an evaluation infrastructure. As BCI reads the implicit state of the user, it allows us to measure alignment failures that are invisible to a keyboard. We can assess persuasion risk by tracking if the model manipulates the user’s attention and emotion. We can measure true comprehension to see if the user actually understood the model’s plan instead of just clicking approve. We can even detect moral discomfort when the model triggers subconscious red flags, or catch overtrust when it induces confidence without actual understanding. This information is very valuable when evaluating a model.
第三，作为评估基础设施。当 BCI 读取用户的隐性状态时，它让我们能够测量键盘看不见的对齐失败。我们可以通过追踪模型是否操纵用户的注意力和情绪来评估说服风险。我们可以测量真正的理解程度，看用户是否真正理解了模型的计划，而不仅仅是点击批准。我们甚至可以在模型触发潜意识红旗时检测到道德不适，或在它诱发缺乏真正理解的自信时捕捉到过度信任。这些信息在评估模型时非常宝贵。
At the current rate, I expect that by the end of 2028, at least one peer-reviewed paper will demonstrate that brain tuning improves a flagship model’s alignment metric on a standardized benchmark.
按照目前的速度，我预期到 2028 年底，至少会有一篇经同行评审的论文证明，脑调控能改善某款旗舰模型在标准化基准上的对齐指标。
Sidenote · 旁注
Now, there is a quick caveat. The human brain is not a perfect blueprint or oversight signal. History shows us that giving a single human infinite power usually ends in a staggering catastrophe, so we do not want to build a superintelligence that perfectly mimics our flaws. Furthermore, our raw neurological reactions are often fast, automatic impulses. These instant reactions are not always the correct ones to capture, as the values we actually want an AI to learn often come from slower, more deliberate thought.46 But as a starting architectural template, the brain is the only working model of aligned general intelligence we have; and aligning an AI to humanity will get a whole lot easier once the field finally understands how “human alignment” actually works.
这里有一个需要迅速说明的限定条件。人脑并不是一幅完美的蓝图或监督信号。历史告诉我们，给单个的人无限的权力，通常会以惊人的灾难收场，所以我们并不想建造一个完美模仿我们缺陷的超级智能。此外，我们原始的神经反应往往是快速、自动的冲动。这些即时反应并不总是应当捕捉的正确对象，因为我们真正希望 AI 学习的价值观，往往来自更慢、更审慎的思考。⟦46⟧ 但作为一份起始的架构模板，大脑是我们拥有的、唯一已经在运作的对齐通用智能模型；一旦这个领域终于理解“人类对齐”实际上是如何运作的，把 AI 与人类对齐就会容易得多。
Paul Berg, who convened Asilomar in 1975. Biologists paused their own research to figure out the safety of recombinant DNA.
保罗·伯格（Paul Berg），他于 1975 年召集了阿西洛马会议（Asilomar）。生物学家暂停了自己的研究，以弄清重组 DNA 的安全性。
The End-Game: Symbiosis
终局：共生
As we move forward, the role of BCI, and neurotechnology, in alignment will evolve across three distinct horizons. In the near-term, it will unlock richer neural feedback, better RLHF, and higher- fidelity evaluations. In the medium-term, it will enable meaningful human-in-the-loop systems, neural scalable oversight, and intent preservation. And in the long-term, it will point toward deeper symbiosis, preserving human agency in a post-AGI world.
随着我们向前推进，BCI 以及神经技术在对齐中的作用将沿着三个不同的视野演化。在近期，它将解锁更丰富的神经反馈、更好的 RLHF 和更高保真的评估。在中期，它将使有意义的“人在回路”系统、神经可扩展监督和意图保全成为可能。而在长期，它将指向更深的共生，在后 AGI 世界中保全人类的能动性。
BCI is a powerful tool to help solve some of the hardest problems in alignment. Right now, we align models by looking at their final outputs and grading them. But what happens when an AGI writes a million-line codebase, or proposes a novel biophysics theory? The human evaluator will be far too slow and limited to know if the AI is right, hallucinating, or actively deceiving us.
BCI 是帮助解决对齐中一些最难题的有力工具。眼下，我们通过对模型的最终输出打分来对齐它们。但当一个 AGI 写出一百万行的代码库，或提出一套全新的生物物理理论时，会发生什么？人类评估者将远太慢、太有限，无法知道 AI 是对的、在产生幻觉，还是在主动欺骗我们。
Over longer horizons, neurotechnology may become the only way to meaningfully oversee a superhuman system. Instead of checking every output or line of code, we can couple directly with AI’s high-level conceptual map, operating at a matched level of abstraction; and ultimately, we can upgrade our biological neurons to match the capabilities of the machines, creating a deeper symbiosis between human and machine. Smartphones have been the primitive version of this symbiosis; BCI and brain augmentation will be a mature evolution of it, through an AI exocortex. In a world with systems a million times more capable than us, high-bandwidth interfaces and brain augmentation are what can keep humans meaningfully around, rather than ceremonially approving decisions we no longer understand.
在更长的时间视野上，神经技术可能成为有意义地监督一个超人类系统的唯一方式。我们不必检查每一个输出或每一行代码，而是可以直接与 AI 的高层概念图耦合，在相匹配的抽象层次上运作；最终，我们可以升级自己的生物神经元以匹配机器的能力，在人与机器之间创造更深的共生。智能手机一直是这种共生的原始版本；BCI 和大脑增强将是它通过 AI 外皮层实现的成熟演化。在一个拥有比我们强大一百万倍的系统的世界里，高带宽界面和大脑增强能够让人类有意义地存在，而不是仪式性地批准我们已不再理解的决定。
If the concept of symbiosis sounds crazy to you, think about it this way: Your brain is not a single, uniform machine, it is already a symbiosis of semi-autonomous sub-systems: a brainstem that runs your heartbeat and breathing, subcortical circuits that fire fear and craving a half-second before reason arrives, and a cortex that narrates the whole thing independently. These systems evolved at different times and run on different logic, yet you do not experience yourself as a committee, you experience a single, unified “I”. Evolution’s way of improving cognition is already to upgrade existing systems and allow new neural networks to integrate into the mind.
如果共生这个概念对你来说听起来很疯狂，可以这样想：你的大脑并不是一台单一、均匀的机器，它已经是多个半自主子系统的共生：一个运行心跳和呼吸的脑干，一些在理性到来前半秒就触发恐惧和渴望的皮层下回路，以及一个独立叙述这一切的皮层。这些系统在不同时期演化，运行着不同的逻辑，但你并不会体验到自己是一个委员会，你体验到的是一个单一、统一的“我”。演化改进认知的方式，已经是升级现有系统，并允许新的神经网络融入心智。
Alignment-by-integration would extend that architecture outward. Rather than keeping AI as a separate agent controlled through a narrow interface, BCI could couple this external cognitive layer into a new cognitive subsystem of the mind. Alignment-by-control keeps the new intelligence outside the self, a separate thing to be watched and constrained. Alignment-by-integration removes the boundary, the same way your cortex and the rest of your brain share no boundary you can feel, a natural extension of Darwinian evolution.
融合式对齐将把这种架构向外延伸。BCI 不是把 AI 保持为一个通过狭窄界面加以控制的分离主体，而是可以把这个外部认知层耦合为心智的一个新认知子系统。控制式对齐把新智能留在自我之外，作为一个被监视和约束的分离之物。融合式对齐则消除了边界，就像你的皮层与大脑其余部分之间没有你可以感觉到的边界一样——这是达尔文式演化的自然延伸。
46. One implication is that neural training data should come from carefully selected people whose considered judgments reflect the values we want the model to learn. This may matter less during pretraining, but could be especially important during fine-tuning.
46. 一个含义是，神经训练数据应来自经过审慎选择的人，其经过深思的判断反映我们希望模型学习的价值观。这在预训练期间可能不那么重要，但在微调期间可能尤为重要。
Sidenote · 旁注
The complete picture of human-AI symbiosis requires the ability to write to the brain, on top of reading from it, and eventually, the ability to upgrade our biological circuitry to match the thinking speed of the AI. But we have intentionally barely touched on these topics because they fall outside the core focus of this manifesto, we will nevertheless dive into them further in Section 20.
人机共生的完整图景，需要在读取大脑之外还具备向大脑写入的能力，并最终具备升级我们的生物回路以匹配 AI 思维速度的能力。但我们有意几乎没有触及这些话题，因为它们超出了本宣言的核心焦点；不过，我们将在第 20 节中进一步探讨它们。
In order to be effective, the human-AI symbiosis must happen at a civilization scale, not just at an individual one. Alignment is a dynamic, pluralistic balancing act, not a static equation to solve. It is never as simple as “align my assistant to me”. True alignment must account for billions of humans, conflicting values, laws, institutions, democratic legitimacy, minority protections, intergenerational interests and non-human conscious beings. While we still do not have a clear plan of how we will handle all those conflicting dimensions, BCI will be a valuable asset in shaping a solution.
要真正有效，人机共生必须发生在文明尺度上，而不仅仅是个人尺度上。对齐是一场动态的、多元的平衡，而不是一个有待求解的静态方程。它从来不是简单的“把我的助手与我对齐”。真正的对齐必须考虑数十亿人类、相互冲突的价值观、法律、制度、民主合法性、少数群体保护、代际利益以及非人类的有意识存在。虽然我们仍没有一个清晰的计划来处理所有这些相互冲突的维度，但 BCI 将是塑造解决方案的宝贵资产。
A natural question that might arise as we talk about collective alignment is: who are we actually aligning to?
当我们谈论集体对齐时，一个自然会出现的问题是：我们究竟在与谁对齐？
In the end, whoever controls the stack will control the target. An AI perfectly aligned via the neural feedback of a small group, a single company, or a nation-state might be problematic in many ways. It would ignore the majority of humans and other conscious beings, giving them no voice into the future. We often treat accidental misalignment as the ultimate threat, but successful alignment to the wrong principal is just as dangerous.
最终，谁控制技术栈，谁就控制目标。一个通过一小群人、一家公司或一个民族国家的神经反馈完美对齐的 AI，可能在许多方面都成问题。它会无视大多数人类和其他有意识的存在，不让他们在未来拥有发言权。我们常常把意外的错位视为终极威胁，但对错误委托人的成功对齐同样危险。
One speculative practical approach to defining an objective function at the scale of humanity would be to sample the neural activity of a large and diversified set of humans continuously, average the semantics of each individual mind over several years to flatten out short term impulses, and finally use that as a loss function to steer ASI. The aim would be to preserve the diversity of individual preferences while distinguishing enduring judgments from momentary impulses using the highest fidelity source we can access, the mind. Such a signal could provide a richer empirical foundation for continuously capturing humanity’s long term goal (and provide a more robust solution to outer alignment47). Crucially, this reference would keep evolving alongside humanity over the decades rather than lock future generations into a fixed snapshot of our values.
一种尚属推测、但可实践的、在人类尺度上定义目标函数的方法，是持续采样一大批多样化人群的神经活动，把每个个体心智的语义在若干年里加以平均以熨平短期冲动，最后把它用作引导人工超级智能（ASI）的损失函数。其目的是在保全个体偏好多样性的同时，利用我们所能获取的最高保真来源——心智——把持久的判断与瞬间的冲动区分开来。这样的信号可以为持续捕捉人类的长期目标提供更丰富的经验基础（并为外在对齐⟦47⟧提供更稳健的方案）。至关重要的是，这一参照会在几十年里与人类一同演化，而不是把未来世代锁定在我们价值观的一个固定快照上。
47. Outer alignment is the problem of giving an AI the right objective in the first place, so that what it is trained to optimize matches what we actually want; inner alignment is the separate problem of making sure the trained model truly pursues that objective.
47. 外在对齐（outer alignment）是首先给 AI 以正确目标的问题，使它所被训练去优化的东西与我们真正想要的相一致；内在对齐（inner alignment）则是另一个问题：确保训练出来的模型真正追求那个目标。
Generally, the premise of most alignment work so far has been to build a separate agent, and then make it obey. How do the slow biological apes constrain the superintelligent digital god? As long as there is an air gap between human and machine, alignment is an adversarial containment game.
总体而言，迄今大多数对齐工作的前提，是先建造一个分离的主体，然后让它服从。缓慢的生物猿类如何约束超级智能的数字之神？只要人与机器之间存在气隙，对齐就是一场对抗性的遏制博弈。
But high-bandwidth BCI and neurotechnology eventually blur the line between the user and the tool. If your cognitive process is seamlessly integrated with the AI’s compute, the AI isn’t an “other” that needs to be aligned. It is just an extension of your own cortex, your own self. You don’t need to “align” your own arm to your brain; it is part of you. At the limit, BCI transforms the alignment problem from a containment problem (alignment-by-control) into an identity problem (alignment-by- integration).
但高带宽 BCI 和神经技术最终会模糊用户与工具之间的界限。如果你的认知过程与 AI 的计算无缝整合，AI 就不再是一个需要被对齐的“他者”。它只是你自己皮层、你自己自我的延伸。你不需要把你自己的手臂与你的大脑“对齐”；它是你的一部分。在极限情况下，BCI 把对齐问题从一个遏制问题（控制式对齐）转变为一个身份问题（融合式对齐）。
It is important to note though that higher bandwidth BCI is not automatically alignment-positive. The same strategy that closes the human-AI bandwidth gap can also be used to create faster manipulation, deeper dependency, and unprecedented surveillance. BCI helps alignment only if paired with strong agency, consent, privacy, and interpretability norms. Like most powerful technologies, it carries dual-use risks. We explore those risks further in Section 22.
不过需要指出的是，更高带宽的 BCI 并不自动有利于对齐。同一套缩小人机带宽差距的策略，也可以被用来制造更快的操纵、更深的依赖和前所未有的监视。只有当 BCI 与强有力的能动性、同意、隐私和可解释性规范相配套时，它才有助于对齐。与大多数强大技术一样，它带有两用风险。我们将在第 22 节进一步探讨这些风险。
Additionally, despite its importance, BCI alone is necessary but not sufficient, it is an important lever, but still only one lever. There is still a lot of uncertainty about what exact role BCI will end up playing in this story. But for all the reasons above, it seems highly probable that whichever scenario ends up unfolding, neurotechnology will have a major impact on the world and it is a tool we will absolutely want to have in our arsenal.
此外，尽管它很重要，但仅有 BCI 是必要而不充分的：它是一根重要的杠杆，但仍然只是其中一根。关于 BCI 最终将在这个故事中扮演何种精确角色，仍有很多不确定性。但基于上述所有理由，无论最终展开的是哪一种情景，神经技术都很可能对世界产生重大影响，它是我们绝对希望纳入武器库的工具。
I am making some strong claims in this section, and across this manifesto, but we now live in a world where decades happen in years. This is not just the extended hallucination of a techno-optimist, it is a thesis grounded in the dizzying new reality we now live in, where technology moves faster than our minds can process. As I write those lines, the Phase Lock Window is actively swinging shut, humanity now has little time to build the necessary infrastructure for the New World.
我在本节以及本宣言中提出了一些强烈的主张，但我们如今生活在一个十年浓缩为几年的世界里。这并不只是一个技术乐观主义者延长了的幻觉，而是一份植根于我们如今所处的令人眩晕的新现实的论文——在这个现实中，技术的移动速度快于我们的心智能处理的速度。在我写下这些文字时，相位锁定窗口正在主动关闭，人类现在几乎没有时间去为新世界建造必要的基础设施。
CHECKPOINT III · 检查点 III
The first two parts defined the trajectory of BCI. Part III examined what happens once it collides with AI.
前两部分定义了 BCI 的轨迹。第三部分考察了它与 AI 碰撞之后会发生什么。
I split it into four sections:
我把它分成四个小节：
Converging Trajectories: We explored how the walls between these two fields are melting. AI is accelerating our ability to decode the brain, while BCI is opening new sources of data and control for AI. Their trajectories are locking together under the immense gravitational pull of AI.
交汇的轨迹：我们探讨了这两个领域之间的墙壁如何正在融化。AI 正在加速我们解码大脑的能力，而 BCI 正在为 AI 打开新的数据来源和控制手段。在 AI 巨大的引力作用下，它们的轨迹正在锁定在一起。
The New Major Modality: Neural data is about to become a new major training modality, giving models access to cognitive signals upstream of text and behaviour. Neural datasets will unlock billions in AI capital into BCI and accelerate the hardware.
新的主要模态：神经数据即将成为一种新的主要训练模态，使模型能够获取位于文本和行为上游的认知信号。神经数据集将为 BCI 解锁数十亿美元的 AI 资本，并加速硬件发展。
The New Interface: BCI could replace the narrow, sequential channels of keyboards and speech with a continuous stream of intent, context, and feedback, fundamentally changing how humans build and direct AI.
新的界面：BCI 可以用意图、情境和反馈的连续数据流，取代键盘和语音那种狭窄、顺序化的通道，从根本上改变人类构建和引导 AI 的方式。
Why This Matters for AI Alignment: Finally, we took a step back and looked at why all of this is critical for our survival. BCI offers several distinct alignment levers: a deeper empirical map of human values to inform model design, a higher- bandwidth channel that keeps human judgment coupled to AI and ultimately, a path towards human neural augmentation and a deeper human-AI symbiosis. Neurotechnology reframes the AI oversight problem from an adversarial game of containment towards a cooperative game of integration.
为什么这对 AI 对齐至关重要：最后，我们退后一步，审视为什么这一切对我们的生存至关重要。BCI 提供了几根不同的对齐杠杆：一幅关于人类价值观的更深层经验地图，为模型设计提供信息；一条更高带宽的通道，使人的判断与 AI 保持耦合；以及最终，一条通向人类神经增强和更深人人机共生的路径。神经技术把 AI 监督问题，从一场遏制的对抗性博弈，重新框架为一场融合的合作性博弈。
PART IV.
第四部分
The Aftermath
余波（The Aftermath）
What are the implications for the future?
对未来意味着什么？
20 Beyond the Read
20 超越读取
Up until now, we have been asking where BCI is going: what role it seems destined to play, why non-invasive reading matters, and why it has a natural synergy with AI. Now we turn the telescope around, instead of asking what BCI is becoming, we ask what kind of future it might create. First, we will zoom out beyond non-invasive reading and look at the rest of neurotechnology. Then we will move through the geopolitical consequences, the ethical minefield, and the strongest counterarguments against this manifesto. Finally, we will look at what the decade ahead holds before reaching the end of our journey.
到目前为止，我们一直在追问 BCI 将走向何方：它似乎注定扮演什么角色，为什么非侵入式读取很重要，以及为什么它与 AI 有着天然的协同。现在我们把望远镜掉转过来：不再问 BCI 正在变成什么，而是问它可能创造怎样的未来。首先，我们将把视野拉出非侵入式读取，审视神经技术的其余部分。然后我们将依次讨论地缘政治后果、伦理雷区和对本宣言最有力的反驳。最后，在旅程结束之前，我们将展望未来十年将带来什么。
Throughout this manifesto, we have been staring at one very specific corner of the map. We focused almost entirely on BCI. Within BCI, almost entirely on non-invasive systems. And within non-invasive systems, almost entirely on reading. In other words, we carved out one narrow slice of the vast neurotechnology landscape and mostly ignored everything else.
在整篇宣言中，我们一直盯着地图上一个非常特定的角落。我们几乎只关注 BCI。在 BCI 之内，几乎只关注非侵入式系统。在非侵入式系统之内，又几乎只关注读取。换句话说，我们从广阔的神经技术版图中切出了一薄片，并大体忽略了其他一切。
This was intentional.
这是有意为之的。
Non-invasive reading is the commercial spark that will ignite the entire wildfire. It will act as a massive magnet for capital, talent, and public attention, dragging a reluctant and fragmented ecosystem into existence. It is upstream of the rest of the field.
非侵入式读取是将点燃整场野火的商业火花。它将像一块巨大的磁铁，吸引资本、人才和公众注意力，把一个不情愿且碎片化的生态系统拖入存在。它位于该领域其余部分的上游。
However, the boundaries across this landscape are incredibly porous. The foundational pipelines built to decode messy, non-invasive waves transfer directly to clean, invasive data arrays. The hardware teams mastering consumer sensor manufacturing at volume are also building the expertise that can eventually produce surgical implants. The acceleration of non-invasive BCI will accelerate the rest of the field.
然而，这片版图上的边界是极其多孔的。为解码杂乱的非侵入式波形而建立的基础管道，可以直接迁移到干净的侵入式数据阵列。那些掌握了消费级传感器大规模制造的硬件团队，也在积累最终能生产手术植入物的专长。非侵入式 BCI 的加速将加速整个领域的其余部分。
Every component of this broader neurotechnology matrix will eventually play a vital role in bridging the human-machine gap, maturing on a trailing timeline. While non-invasive reading is starting to hit escape velocity today, I expect consumer-grade write capabilities to follow in roughly five years, fully invasive BCI to hit true scale in nine years, whole brain emulation (WBE) to arrive within a 15- year horizon and neural augmentation within 20 years.
这一更广阔的神经技术矩阵中的每一个组成部分，最终都将在弥合人机差距中扮演关键角色，只是成熟的时间会滞后。非侵入式读取今天已开始达到逃逸速度，我预期消费级写入能力大约在五年后跟进，全侵入式 BCI 在九年后达到真正的规模，全脑仿真（WBE）在 15 年的视野内到来，神经增强则在 20 年内到来。
Each of those technologies will play a part in addressing the two major bottlenecks of the mind: how effectively we communicate and how quickly we can think. Over the next few years, BCI promises to substantially improve the communication layer, but ultimately, the biological limits of cognition will remain. Even a perfect brain-AI connection would not let a biological brain keep pace with a superintelligence. Addressing this second bottleneck means expanding cognition itself. This is where the broader neurotechnology field comes into play: neural augmentation and whole brain emulation could extend human cognitive capabilities beyond their biological limits, helping close the gap that better communication alone cannot bridge. This manifesto focuses on non-invasive BCI because it offers the most practical starting point, but neurotechnology’s role in the transition to AGI will reach beyond interfaces.
这些技术中的每一项，都将在应对心智的两大瓶颈上发挥作用：我们沟通的有效程度，以及我们思考的速度。在未来几年里，BCI 有望实质性改善沟通层，但最终，认知的生物学极限仍将存在。即便是完美的脑—AI 连接，也无法让一个生物大脑跟上超级智能。应对这第二个瓶颈，意味着扩展认知本身。这正是更广义的神经技术发挥作用的地方：神经增强和全脑仿真可以将人类认知能力延伸到生物学极限之外，帮助弥合仅靠更好沟通无法跨越的差距。本宣言聚焦于非侵入式 BCI，是因为它提供了最务实的起点，但神经技术在向 AGI 过渡中的作用将超越界面。
Let’s now explore the blind spots in neurotechnology that we have mostly ignored so far:
现在让我们探讨迄今为止大多被我们忽略的神经技术盲点：
Writing to the Brain
向大脑写入
For a true human-machine merge, reading the brain is only half the picture. The symbiosis we mapped earlier, where an AI seamlessly interacts with you at a higher conceptual abstraction level, implicitly relies on a two-way street. In the short term, the machine will just talk back to you through an earpiece or an AR display. But eventually, true symbiosis will require the ability to write directly back into the cortex.
对于真正的人机融合而言，读取大脑只是图景的一半。我们前面描绘的那种共生——AI 在更高的概念抽象层次上与你无缝交互——隐含地依赖于一条双向通道。短期内，机器只会通过耳机或 AR 显示屏对你回话。但最终，真正的共生将需要直接向皮层写回的能力。
Writing is a fundamentally different, and much harder, physics problem.
写入是一个根本不同、也困难得多的物理问题。
When we read the brain, we exploit a natural leakage. Neural activity casts faint electrical, magnetic, and hemodynamic shadows through the skull, which our sensors can catch. Stimulating a specific neuronal population through a closed skull, at the precision required to cause a thought rather than disrupt one, is a fundamentally different physical problem than reading. Writing does not have the same leakage in reverse: there is no equivalent path that lets you push a precise pattern into the cortex. Today, our write capabilities are poor. We can broadly stimulate large clusters of neurons, but we are far from elegantly injecting a specific, fine-grained thought. The brain is wildly chaotic, and altering it precisely from the outside remains an immense engineering hurdle.
当我们读取大脑时，我们利用的是一种自然的泄漏。神经活动透过颅骨投下微弱的电、磁和血流动力学“影子”，我们的传感器可以捕捉到它们。而要透过闭合的颅骨刺激一个特定的神经元群，并精确到能够引发一个想法而非扰乱它，则是一个与读取根本不同的物理问题。写入并不具备同样的反向泄漏：没有一条对等的路径让你把一个精确的模式推入皮层。今天，我们的写入能力很差。我们可以粗略地刺激大簇神经元，但距离优雅地注入一个特定的、细粒度的想法还很远。大脑极度混乱，从外部精确改变它仍然是一道巨大的工程障碍。
But the write side of BCI is already making progress. Crude forms of non-invasive brain stimulation are already being used clinically to treat depression, OCD, and other circuit-level conditions. And within a few years, as the massive influx of capital and talent from the reading side of BCI spills over, we will see an acute acceleration of the development of brain writing technologies.
但 BCI 的写入一侧已经在取得进展。粗糙形式的非侵入式脑刺激已经在临床上被用于治疗抑郁症、强迫症（OCD）以及其他回路层面的病症。再过几年，随着读取一侧 BCI 带来的资本和人才大量溢出，我们将看到脑写入技术的发展急剧加速。
Invasive BCI
侵入式 BCI
Beyond the write aspect, we have also spent most of this manifesto focused squarely on non-invasive hardware. Interacting with the brain from outside of the skull. But as we established earlier on the capability ladder, non-invasive technology has a hard, physical ceiling. The human skull is essentially a massive biological dampening armor. Trying to read or write through it makes everything a hundred times harder.
除了写入这一面，本宣言的大部分篇幅也几乎完全聚焦于非侵入式硬件——从颅骨外部与大脑交互。但正如我们前面在能力阶梯上所确立的，非侵入式技术有一个坚硬的物理上限。人类的颅骨本质上是一套巨大的生物阻尼装甲。试图透过它读取或写入，会使一切都难上一百倍。
The ultimate, terminal form factor of BCI will absolutely be invasive. It will just take longer to get there.
BCI 的终极、终端形态绝对会是侵入式的。只是到达那里需要更长的时间。
This is the playground of companies like Neuralink, Synchron, Paradromics, Precision Neuroscience. By clearing the dura mater and placing electrodes directly on or inside the brain, the physical noise vanishes. The signal in both directions increases by orders of magnitude compared to anything that has to pass through bone.
这是 Neuralink、Synchron、Paradromics、Precision Neuroscience 这类公司的竞技场。通过穿过硬脑膜、把电极直接放在大脑上或大脑内，物理噪声便消失了。两个方向上的信号，都比任何必须穿过骨骼的方案高出几个数量级。
We already know what reading at this fidelity looks like. In a landmark 2021 Stanford study, a paralyzed patient mentally “handwrote” text at 90 characters per minute simply by imagining the motor movements, all captured by an intracortical array. That is the kind of pristine, high-resolution signal you get when you remove the skull from the equation. And that is what will eventually produce the cleanest, highest-fidelity brain interface in the long term.
我们已经知道这种保真度下的读取是什么样子。在斯坦福大学 2021 年一项具有里程碑意义的研究中，一名瘫痪患者仅凭想象运动动作，就以每分钟 90 个字符的速度在心里“手写”出文本，全部由皮层内阵列捕捉。这就是当你把颅骨从方程中去掉时所能得到的那种纯净、高分辨率的信号。而这最终将在长期内造就最干净、最高保真的脑接口。
Invasive hardware will reach consumer scale last because neurosurgery is the ultimate regulatory and psychological bottleneck. But when it finally arrives, it will carry the absolute highest capability ceiling. And the rapid advances in gene editing and nanotechnology may bring it to us in forms we have yet to imagine, potentially bypassing surgery altogether.48
侵入式硬件将最晚达到消费级规模，因为神经外科是终极的监管和心理瓶颈。但当它最终到来时，它将拥有绝对最高的能力上限。而基因编辑和纳米技术的快速进步，可能会以我们尚未想象到的形态把它带给我们，甚至有可能完全绕过手术。⟦48⟧
48. If you wish to learn more about invasive brain decoding, this is a good talk on the topic. It breaks down the Stanford study where a BCI decoded handwriting movements into text at record speeds.
48. 如果你想进一步了解侵入式脑解码，这是一场很好的相关讲座。它剖析了斯坦福那项把书写动作解码为文本、并创下速度纪录的 BCI 研究。
Whole Brain Emulation
全脑仿真
Taking another step back, there is another area of neurotechnology that we have not discussed yet, but which will certainly play a major role in the future: Whole Brain Emulation.
再退后一步，还有一个我们尚未讨论、但必将在未来扮演重要角色的神经技术领域：全脑仿真（Whole Brain Emulation, WBE）。
WBE is exactly what it sounds like: the concept of emulating a biological brain directly inside a computer. While this has the distinct ring of science fiction, it is actively transitioning into a concrete engineering discipline. We have already made huge progress in digitally mapping and emulating the entire brains of animals. In late 2024, the FlyWire consortium successfully mapped the complete connectome of an adult fruit fly, detailing the exact wiring of nearly 140,000 neurons and over 50 million synapses.
WBE 正如其名：在计算机内部直接仿真一个生物大脑的概念。尽管这明显带有科幻色彩，它正在积极转变为一个具体的工程学科。我们在数字测绘和仿真动物的整个大脑方面已经取得了巨大进展。2024 年底，FlyWire 联盟成功绘制出一只成年果蝇的完整连接组，详细描绘了近 14 万个神经元和超过 5,000 万个突触的精确接线。
A complete fly brain: In October 2024, researchers published the first complete connectome of an adult fruit fly. Source: FlyWire.
一个完整的果蝇大脑：2024 年 10 月，研究人员发表了第一份成年果蝇的完整连接组。来源：FlyWire。
Sidenote · 旁注
In one early demonstration, researchers took a fly’s brain data, turned it into an emulated neural circuit, placed that circuit inside a virtual fly body, and let it drive behavior. It moved without a hand-written control system, the virtual mind managed to generate movements directly from the reconstructed brain attempting to run a virtual body.
在一次早期演示中，研究人员拿来一只果蝇的脑数据，把它变成一个仿真的神经回路，把这个回路放入一个虚拟果蝇身体中，并让它驱动行为。它在没有手写控制系统的情况下运动，虚拟心智成功地从被重建的大脑中直接生成动作，尝试驱动一个虚拟身体。
Whole brain emulation is essentially the other branch of the neurotechnology tree. Much of the capital, talent, regulatory infrastructure, and hardware flowing into BCI also accelerates WBE because the two fields share core dependencies: high-resolution neural sensing, models trained on brain data, and standardized methods for mapping the brain.
全脑仿真本质上是神经技术之树的另一分支。流入 BCI 的大量资本、人才、监管基础设施和硬件，也会加速 WBE，因为这两个领域共享核心依赖：高分辨率神经传感、在脑数据上训练的模型，以及测绘大脑的标准化方法。
WBE is also a candidate path to artificial general intelligence. A working human emulation is, by construction, at least human-level intelligence, but with the added ability to run faster, operate in parallel, and duplicate at digital scale. Most reasonable forecasts conclude that WBE is unlikely to arrive before native AI systems achieve AGI, but the underlying economics are shifting. A recent thesis evaluating whether whole brain emulation will matter for the AI transition puts the all-in cost of a human emulation at roughly $50 billion in current scanning and compute terms.49
WBE 也是通往通用人工智能的一条候选路径。一个可运作的人类仿真，就其建构而言，至少是人类水平的智能，但额外具备更快运行、并行运作，以及以数字规模复制的能力。大多数合理的预测认为，WBE 不太可能在原生 AI 系统实现 AGI 之前到来，但其底层经济学正在发生变化。一篇评估全脑仿真是否将在 AI 转变中发挥作用的近期论文，按当前扫描和计算成本估算，一次人类仿真的全部成本约为 500 亿美元。⟦49⟧
If non-invasive BCI scales exactly the way this manifesto argues it will, the cost of neural data acquisition and processing will plummet. As a result, the WBE timeline will compress dramatically right alongside it.50
如果非侵入式 BCI 确实按照本宣言所论证的方式规模化，神经数据采集和处理的成本将急剧下降。结果是，WBE 的时间线也将随之大幅压缩。⟦50⟧
One possible application of WBE for AI alignment would be to use it for supervision at machine speed. A sufficiently accelerated emulation could follow an AI’s actions, evaluate them against the person’s preferences, and intervene while its biological counterpart remains outside the immediate interaction. It could then report back at a pace the person can follow. This would offer a potential way to address the cognitive bottleneck described earlier.
WBE 用于 AI 对齐的一种可能应用，是以机器速度进行监督。一个足够加速的仿真可以跟踪 AI 的行动，对照此人的偏好加以评估，并在其生物对应者仍处于即时交互之外时进行干预。然后它可以以这个人跟得上的节奏汇报回来。这将提供一种应对前面所述认知瓶颈的潜在方式。
49. For more on WBE’s relevance to the AI transition, see this blog post.
49. 关于 WBE 与 AI 转变的相关性，见这篇博文。
50. For those wanting to dig deeper into how close we are to turning biological brains into digital substrates, you can explore the roadmap from a 2025 Neurotechnology Workshop, which gathered leading researchers.
50. 对于想更深入了解我们距离把生物大脑变成数字基质还有多近的人，可以参阅 2025 年神经技术研讨会的路线图，该研讨会汇集了领先的研究者。
Neural Augmentation
神经增强
Looking further into the future, neurotechnology could unlock the possibility of expanding cognition itself. Neural augmentation could enhance or eventually replace parts of our biological circuitry, increasing the speed and capacity of thought. We could make the human mind itself more capable, allowing us to participate more natively in a world operating at the speed of machines.
再向更远的未来看，神经技术可能解锁扩展认知本身的可能性。神经增强可以增强或最终替换我们部分生物回路，提高思维的速度和容量。我们可以让人类心智本身变得更有能力，使我们能够更原生地参与一个以机器速度运转的世界。
Neural augmentation is still a long way from where we stand today, but given the trajectory of the field, I expect it to become a reality within two decades, making mind augmentation one of the key levers for long term alignment-by-integration.
神经增强距离我们今天所处的位置仍然很远，但鉴于该领域的轨迹，我预期它将在二十年之内成为现实，从而使心智增强成为长期融合式对齐的关键杠杆之一。
Sidenote · 旁注
At the limit of our neural sci-fi journey lies mind uploading, the possibility of recreating a person’s memories, personality, and cognitive processes in a digital substrate. A sufficiently faithful emulation could, in principle, carry aspects of who we are beyond the limits of biology. Whether it could be done while preserving the original person’s “consciousness” or whether it would create a new “conscious”51 individual remains an open question.
在我们这场神经科幻旅程的极限处，是心智上传：在数字基质中重建一个人的记忆、个性和认知过程的可能性。一个足够忠实的仿真，原则上可以把我们是谁的某些方面带到生物学极限之外。它是否能在保全原初之人的“意识”的同时完成，还是会创造出一个新的“有意识的”⟦51⟧个体，仍然是一个开放的问题。
But let us come back to the present, where there are still countless challenges to making these visions turn into a reality. Up to this point, we have treated BCI, and neurotechnology, mostly as pure scientific and engineering equations, a matter of physics, compute, and biological ceilings. Yet, technology does not scale in a vacuum, it scales in a messy reality driven by capital, power, and state interests. Let us now turn to the geopolitical forces taking shape around BCI.
但让我们回到当下，要把这些愿景变成现实，仍有无数挑战。到目前为止，我们大多把 BCI 以及神经技术当作纯粹的科学和工程方程来对待——物理学、算力和生物学上限的问题。然而，技术并不是在真空中规模化的，它是在由资本、权力和国家利益驱动的杂乱现实中规模化的。现在让我们转向正在 BCI 周围成形的地缘政治力量。
21 Geopolitical Games
21 地缘政治博弈
To fully understand how the BCI scenario will unfold, we have to step out of the lab and look at the broader chessboard. As the strategic value of the human-machine merge becomes undeniable, private markets and sovereign states are slowly taking their positions. The race for the ultimate neurotechnology is no longer just a niche scientific endeavor, it is becoming an economic and geopolitical reality.
要充分理解 BCI 情景将如何展开，我们必须走出实验室，看看更广阔的棋盘。随着人机融合的战略价值变得不可否认，私人市场和主权国家正在慢慢就位。对终极神经技术的竞赛不再只是一项小众的科学事业，它正在成为经济与地缘政治的现实。
In the past few decades, the dominance of a state, a corporation, or an economic hub has been in large part defined by its mastery over the prevailing technological paradigm. While the coming decades will undoubtedly be defined by artificial intelligence, the interfaces connecting humans to that intelligence will play just as critical a role. Some global powers are starting to recognize this emerging reality and the key role BCI will play over the coming decades.
在过去几十年里，一个国家、一家公司或一个经济枢纽的主导地位，在很大程度上由其对该时代主流技术范式的掌握所定义。未来几十年无疑将由人工智能所定义，但把人类连接到那种智能的界面，将扮演同样关键的角色。一些全球大国正开始认识到这一正在浮现的现实，以及 BCI 在未来几十年将扮演的关键角色。
BCI’s increased geopolitical strategic importance will develop over four main phases, with a sharp jump in interest at each step:
BCI 地缘政治战略重要性的提升将经历四个主要阶段，每一步都会出现兴趣的急剧跃升：
The Clinical Foundation: Treating paralysis, stroke, epilepsy, and blindness. This is the bulk of BCI as of 2026.
临床基础：治疗瘫痪、中风、癫痫和失明。这是截至 2026 年 BCI 的主体。
Productivity and Control: Hands-free computing, spatial interface navigation, silent input, and real-time cognitive workload management.
