As she blends a shadow on the canvas, she casually executes a technique she never consciously practiced, guided by the vivid memory of a text she never opened.
下午晚些时候，玛雅从数字心流中抽身去画画。她最近培养了这个爱好，尽管学习过程看起来与十年前完全不同。她没有花几个月在透视或色彩理论上失败。相反，她通过神经节点直接获得了基础的肌肉记忆和空间理解。在等一层油彩变干时，她的思绪飘向一本关于视觉美学的厚重哲学书。她实际上从未坐下来读过它，只是在周末接受了概念传输，到了早晨，那些论证对她已经完全可读。当她在画布上调和一处阴影时，她随手执行了一种她从未有意识地练习过的技法，由一本她从未打开过的书的鲜明记忆所引导。
Sitting back, Maya spots a scattering of cups and streamers in the garden from her children’s birthday party yesterday. With a fleeting pulse of intent, she directs a small fleet of domestic robots to clear the lawn. The machines glide into motion as she slips seamlessly back into her afternoon flow, lost in her own thoughts.
坐回去时，玛雅看到花园里散落着孩子们昨天生日聚会留下的杯子和彩带。带着一缕转瞬即逝的意图脉冲，她指挥一小队家用机器人清理草坪。机器们滑入运动，而她无缝地滑回下午的心流，沉浸在自己的思绪中。
Later, she enters the design phase of a new architectural project; she needs to finish the first iteration by tonight. She does not write code or draft blueprints, software and structure have stopped being typed; they are simply imagined into existence. She envisions the layout, materiality, and light of a new building with the fluid, intuitive ease of a child building in Minecraft75.
稍后，她进入一个新建筑项目的设计阶段；她需要在今晚完成第一版迭代。她不写代码，也不起草蓝图，软件和结构已经不再被敲打出来；它们只是被想象进存在。她以一个孩子在《我的世界》（Minecraft）⟦75⟧ 中搭建时那种流畅、直觉的轻松，构想一座新建筑的布局、材质和光线。
By early evening, when her partner walks into the room, Maya does not need to ask how his day went. She can literally feel it; she instantly catches the raw texture of his emotional state, the lingering friction of a difficult morning dissolving into relief as he sits down. The neural link creates a silent, high-bandwidth empathy, allowing them to share the exact form of a feeling without relying on the lossy compression of words.
傍晚时分，当她的伴侣走进房间时，玛雅不需要问他这一天过得怎么样。她能够从字面上感受到；她瞬间捕捉到他情绪状态的原始质地，一个艰难早晨残留的摩擦，在他坐下时溶解成释然。神经连接创造了一种无声的、高带宽的共情，使他们能够在不依赖词语那种有损压缩的情况下，分享一种感受的精确形态。
Lately, the technology has pushed into territory that used to be strictly metaphysical. Last night, her friend, a fervent alpinist, streamed an execution of her ascent of Mount Everest to Maya’s son, who loves mountaineering. He did not just watch a video; he sensorially teleported. Through the shared neural experience, he felt the crisp altitude, the biting cold, and the sharp awe of looking down from the ridge. Memory, and even emotion, have stopped being constrained to first-person acquisition.
近来，这项技术已经推进到曾经严格属于形而上学的领地。昨晚，她的一位热衷登山的朋友，把攀登珠穆朗玛峰的一次执行过程串流给了玛雅热爱登山的儿子。他不只是看了一段视频；他在感官上瞬间传送了。通过共享的神经体验，他感受到了清冽的海拔、刺骨的寒冷，以及从山脊向下望去时那种锐利的敬畏。记忆，甚至情绪，已经不再被约束于第一人称的获取。
75. For those living in a cave: Minecraft is the best-selling video game of all time, built around placing blocks to construct anything you can imagine.
75. 对于那些生活在洞穴里的人：《我的世界》（Minecraft）是有史以来最畅销的电子游戏，围绕放置方块来建造任何你可以想象的东西而构建。
A summit you never climbed, remembered as if you had.
一座你从未攀登过的顶峰，被记得如同你曾经登过。
Even her own past has become fully legible. On her break, Maya occasionally replays her own childhood memories at full resolution; the full experience decoded from the original neural trace and rendered back into her sensorium. She can relive a specific morning from her childhood, or re-experience a conversation with someone who is long gone, catching the exact texture of their voice.
就连她自己的过去也变得完全可读。休息时，玛雅偶尔会以全分辨率回放自己的童年记忆；完整的体验从原始神经轨迹中被解码，再渲染回她的感觉世界。她可以重温童年某个特定的早晨，或重新体验与一个早已离去之人的一次对话，捕捉到其声音的精确质地。
Only in retrospect did it become clear that a threshold had been crossed. While it was happening, every advance looked incremental, and every adoption looked like another ordinary personal choice. There was no master switch, and no dramatic Thursday when everyone plugged into the Matrix.
只有事后回望，才变得清楚：一道门槛已经被跨越。当事情正在发生时，每一次进步都看起来是渐进的，每一次采用都看起来像又一次普通的个人选择。没有总开关，也没有戏剧性的某个星期四，所有人都接入了“母体”（the Matrix）。
The future took hold quietly, as it usually does. First the device was a curiosity, then the norm, then it became strange to live without, and finally it vanished altogether from attention, like electricity or the internet a few decades prior.
未来通常就是这样悄然占据上风的。起初设备是一种新奇之物，然后成为常态，然后没有它生活会显得奇怪，最后它完全从注意力中消失，就像几十年前的电或互联网一样。
A Busy Mote of Dust: Earth, captured from six billion kilometers out by Voyager 1 in 1990.
一粒忙碌的尘埃：地球，由旅行者 1 号（Voyager 1）于 1990 年从 60 亿公里外拍摄。
25 Conclusion
25 结语
For the first time in the history of the known universe, a general intelligence other than our own has emerged. Our long solitude is finally ending. Matter arranged by human hands has begun to think.
在已知宇宙的历史上，第一次出现了另一种不同于我们自身的通用智能。我们漫长的孤独终于即将结束。由人手排布的物质已经开始思考。
With this comes the greatest challenge in the history of our species: artificial intelligence. It forces us to answer impossible questions on an engineering timeline: What is being human when the boundary of the mind becomes permeable? What should we become now that the future is an explicit design choice? And what is our role in the universe that follows?
随之而来的，是我们这个物种历史上最大的挑战：人工智能。它迫使我们以工程的时间尺度去回答不可能的问题：当心智的边界变得可渗透时，作为人意味着什么？既然未来是一个明确的设计选择，我们应当成为什么？而在随后那个宇宙中，我们的角色又是什么？
Reality is bending, and AI is distorting our society faster than we can adapt. We are entering a world governed by systems we did not evolve to understand, operating at speeds we were never built to match. And whether you welcome it or dread it, this new age is already upon us.
现实正在弯曲，AI 扭曲我们社会的速度超过了我们适应的速度。我们正在进入一个由我们并未演化去理解的系统所治理的世界，这些系统以我们从未被建造去匹配的速度运作。无论你欢迎它还是惧怕它，这个新时代已经降临。
But its trajectory is not yet fixed. The coming decade is our remaining chance to shape the future before AI capabilities move beyond our reach, before the Phase Lock Window closes permanently.
但它的轨迹尚未固定。未来十年是我们在 AI 能力超出我们触及范围之前、在相位锁定窗口永久关闭之前，塑造未来所剩的机会。
This manifesto began with a mismatch: machine intelligence is scaling faster than the human capacity to oversee it. And as this bandwidth gap is widening, BCI has emerged as one of the few technologies capable of addressing it at its roots.
本宣言始于一种错配：机器智能的规模化速度快于人类监督它的能力。随着这一带宽差距扩大，BCI 已经成为少数能够从根源上应对它的技术之一。
As of 2026, BCI is still nascent and lagging behind AI, but the path forward is becoming legible: better hardware, larger neural datasets, and bigger models are bringing within reach the breakthroughs needed to move neurotechnology beyond the lab. And as BCI matures, its convergence with AI will shape the trajectory of our civilization.
截至 2026 年，BCI 仍然处于萌芽阶段并落后于 AI，但前进的道路正变得清晰可见：更好的硬件、更大的神经数据集和更大的模型，正在使神经技术走出实验室所需的突破变得触手可及。而随着 BCI 的成熟，它与 AI 的汇合将塑造我们文明的轨迹。
Ultimately, AI will scale BCI, and BCI will scale AI.
最终，AI 将推动 BCI 的规模化，BCI 也将推动 AI 的规模化。
BCI will not be sufficient on its own to solve the numerous challenges we face, but through alignment- by-integration, it will be an important and necessary part of any durable solution. As alignment-by- control becomes ever more fragile, it will create a channel for oversight that scales with the intelligence it supervises, and, in time, the foundation of a symbiosis between biological and artificial minds. In a world increasingly shaped by AI, it tilts the balance of power back toward human intent, ensuring that the systems we build remain instruments of human consciousness and serve the flourishing of life.
仅靠 BCI 并不足以解决我们面临的众多挑战，但通过融合式对齐，它将成为任何持久解决方案中重要且必要的一部分。随着控制式对齐变得越来越脆弱，它将创造一条与其所监督的智能同步规模化的监督通道，并最终成为生物心智与人工心智之间共生的基础。在一个日益由 AI 塑造的世界里，它把力量的天平重新倾向人类意图，确保我们所建造的系统仍然是人类意识的工具，并服务于生命的繁荣。
But the future will not be prosperous by default, we will have to build it deliberately. And the task of forging it does not belong to some abstract, distant generation; it falls upon all of us, right now, and above all on those aware enough to see what is coming.
但未来不会默认繁荣，我们必须有意地去建造它。而锻造它的任务并不属于某个抽象、遥远的世代；它落在我们所有人身上，就在此刻，尤其落在那些足够清醒、能看见即将到来之事的人身上。
If you are reading this, you are one of them.
如果你正在读到这里，你就是其中之一。
Find where you can push, and push. Building for the Old World is mostly a waste of time at this point; all that truly matters now is ensuring humanity can prosper in the age of AI. The work to be done extends beyond BCI76, and I encourage you to figure out how you can take part in shaping this future. Whether you are a builder, an investor or simply someone who cares, feel free to reach out to me with any thoughts and ideas; and if this manifesto has changed how you see the next decade, put it in front of someone who should be helping shape it.
找到你可以推动的地方，然后去推动。到了这个时候，为旧世界建造大多是在浪费时间；现在真正重要的，是确保人类能够在 AI 时代繁荣。有待完成的工作超出了 BCI⟦76⟧，我鼓励你去弄清自己如何能够参与塑造这一未来。无论你是建设者、投资者，还是只是一个在乎的人，都欢迎就任何想法与我联系；如果本宣言改变了你对未来十年的看法，就把它放到那些本应帮助塑造它的人面前。
AI will be humanity’s last prowess, the final expression of our ingenuity before machines take over the instruments of creation. Neurotechnology will help ensure that as they do, humanity remains a composer of what comes next, rather than a fading echo within it.
AI 将是人类最后的非凡才能，是机器接管创造工具之前、我们才智的最后一次表达。神经技术将帮助确保：当它们这样做时，人类仍然是随后之事的作曲者，而不是其中一段正在消退的回声。
As Humanity and AI enter Phase Lock, the choices we make over the next decade will define the centuries to come.
当人类与 AI 进入相位锁定时，我们在未来十年做出的选择将定义未来数个世纪的走向。
The path ahead remains unmarked, the map unfinished, and the horizon unlike anything our species has seen before.
前方的道路仍未标明，地图尚未完成，地平线也不像我们这个物种以前见过的任何东西。
But still, we advance, wading through the fog, drawn by the light of an unwritten tomorrow.
但我们仍然前进，涉过迷雾，被一道尚未写下的明天的光所吸引。
The road ahead: Wading through the fog, drawn by the light of an unwritten tomorrow.
前方的路：涉过迷雾，被一道尚未写下的明天的光所吸引。
76. Here is a concise but complete mental model of what the AGI transition encompasses. You can like the Tweet to be added to a group chat with fellow readers who had the courage to complete this manifesto.
76. 这里有一份简明而完整的、关于 AGI 转变所涵盖内容的思维模型。你可以给这条推文点赞，以被加入一个群聊，与那些有勇气读完本宣言的读者同行。
Thanks for reading to the end, if you found this manifesto insightful and think it is worth passing on, you can do so by reposting the original release post on X
感谢你读到最后。如果你觉得本宣言有洞见、值得传递，你可以通过在 X 上转发最初的发布帖来这样做。

附录
Predictions, acknowledgments, resources, and the modality map.
预测、致谢、资源，以及模态图谱。

我在本宣言中做了若干预测。本附录把主要的预测集中在一处，每一条都带有目标年份和置信度，按目标年份分组并按主题标注。我将随着现实的展开更新它们。

Appendix B: Friends and Thought Partners
附录 B：朋友与思想伙伴
This manifesto did not come to life in a vacuum. The people below have shaped how I see this field over years of conversation. Each, at some point, moved the thinking forward. The smart claims are theirs; the errors and overclaims are mine.
本宣言并非在真空中诞生。下面这些人在多年的交谈中塑造了我对这一领域的看法。他们每个人都曾在某个时刻推动了思考向前。那些聪明的主张属于他们；错误和过度宣称则属于我。
One person holds a special position here: my partner Olya, who read every draft, caught what did not work, and refused to let this sit in my backlog for another two years. It exists in finished form thanks to her.
有一个人在这里占据特殊位置：我的伴侣奥莉娅（Olya），她读过每一稿，捕捉到那些行不通的地方，并拒绝让这份文稿在我的待办事项里再躺上两年。它能以完成的形态存在，要感谢她。

On conveners and community: Diana Saville at BrainMind, Allison Duettmann at the Foresight Institute, and Margot Hanley at NeuroNYC, who bring much of this field together.
召集者与社区方面：BrainMind 的 Diana Saville，Foresight Institute 的 Allison Duettmann，以及 NeuroNYC 的 Margot Hanley，他们把这个领域的很大一部分人聚在一起。
On the outside view: Karim Beguir at Instadeep and Raphael Volpert at Alexandria, Yash Shevde at Ursa Bio, Ansh Chopra at ZeroBillion, Viktor Bowallius at ThinkIF Technology, William Knottenbelt at Imperial College London, Adrian Johnston at Elyos AI, Mete Polat at Varto, Atlal Boudir at Louise, Daniel Faggella at Emerj, Rodolfo Rosini at Vaire Computing, Nikolas Ioannou at Hyper, Dan Girshovich at Tools for Humanity, and Auguste Pugnet, who do not build in neurotech but have been valuable thought partners nonetheless.
外部视角方面：Instadeep 的 Karim Beguir 和 Alexandria 的 Raphael Volpert，Ursa Bio 的 Yash Shevde，ZeroBillion 的 Ansh Chopra，ThinkIF Technology 的 Viktor Bowallius，伦敦帝国理工学院的 William Knottenbelt，Elyos AI 的 Adrian Johnston，Varto 的 Mete Polat，Louise 的 Atlal Boudir，Emerj 的 Daniel Faggella，Vaire Computing 的 Rodolfo Rosini，Hyper 的 Nikolas Ioannou，Tools for Humanity 的 Dan Girshovich，以及 Auguste Pugnet；他们并不在神经技术领域从事建设，但仍然是宝贵的思想伙伴。
Appendix C: Further Resources
附录 C：延伸资源
I mentioned a lot of companies and labs throughout the essay. I am consolidating the ones worth keeping an eye on here, for anyone wanting a bird’s-eye view of the field.
我在全文中提到了许多公司和实验室。我在此把值得持续关注的那些汇总起来，供任何想要鸟瞰这一领域的人参考。
Companies and Labs Worth Watching
值得关注的公司与实验室
Non-invasive hardware: Cerca Magnetics (OP-MEG), Kernel (fNIRS), Nudge (ultrasound), Butterfly Network (ultrasound chip), Aleph Neuro (ultrasound), Sonera (biomagnetic neural sensing).
非侵入式硬件：Cerca Magnetics（OP-MEG）、Kernel（fNIRS）、Nudge（超声）、Butterfly Network（超声芯片）、Aleph Neuro（超声）、Sonera（生物磁性神经传感）。
Non-invasive data and decoding: AllJoined, AE Studio, Mind Co, Neurable, Brill Neurotech, Wisear, Orbit, Conduit, Piramidal, Constellation, Sabi, Xtropi (stealth).
非侵入式数据与解码：AllJoined、AE Studio、Mind Co、Neurable、Brill Neurotech、Wisear、Orbit、Conduit、Piramidal、Constellation、Sabi、Xtropi（隐身中）。
Neural data for AI: Inductive, Metamorphic, Enigma.
用于 AI 的神经数据：Inductive、Metamorphic、Enigma。
Open hardware and community: OpenBCI.
开放硬件与社区：OpenBCI。
Sub-vocal and peripheral neural interface: CTRL-labs (now Meta neural interface): the EMG sub-vocal/intent path that is the closest non-invasive cousin to BCI in the consumer interface frame.
默读与周围神经接口：CTRL-labs（现为 Meta neural interface）：肌电（EMG）默读/意图路径，是消费级界面框架中与 BCI 最接近的非侵入式表亲。
Prior consumer non-invasive wave (mostly cautionary): Muse, Emotiv, NextMind (acquired and then wound down by Snap).
上一波消费级非侵入浪潮（大多具有警示意义）：Muse、Emotiv、NextMind（被 Snap 收购后关停）。
Neuromodulation and stimulation: Sanmai Technologies, META-BRAIN (EU magnetoelectric + ultrasound neuromodulation), Integral Neuroscience.
神经调控与刺激：Sanmai Technologies、META-BRAIN（欧盟磁电 + 超声神经调控）、Integral Neuroscience。
Minimally invasive and invasive: NeuroPace, Paradromics, Synchron, Neuralink, Precision Neuroscience, Starfish Neuroscience, Neurobionics.
微创与侵入式：NeuroPace、Paradromics、Synchron、Neuralink、Precision Neuroscience、Starfish Neuroscience、Neurobionics。
Adjacent and meta: Psyche, Forest Neurotech (now Merge), Inner Cosmos, Cognixion, Lymbic AI, Clarity, Cortical Labs.
相邻与元层面：Psyche、Forest Neurotech（现为 Merge）、Inner Cosmos、Cognixion、Lymbic AI、Clarity、Cortical Labs。
Adjacent, endovascular, and emerging: ivec, Araya Inc., Neuradaptive, Braingrade, Synlinx (China’s national BCI incubator, Shanghai).
相邻、血管内与新兴：ivec、Araya Inc.、Neuradaptive、Braingrade、Synlinx（中国国家 BCI 孵化器，上海）。
Whole brain emulation: Eon, Eightsix Science, Netholabs.
全脑仿真：Eon、Eightsix Science、Netholabs。
Investors and supporting capital: Amaranth Foundation, Jumpspace Ventures, Lionheart Ventures, e184.
投资者与支持资本：Amaranth Foundation、Jumpspace Ventures、Lionheart Ventures、e184。

除公司之外，这里还有一份写作清单，供任何想更深入进入神经技术的人参考：
Further Reading
延伸阅读
Companion essays
配套文章
By myself, Humanity’s Last Prowess. My overarching framework of what matters for the AGI transition.
本人所写，《人类最后的非凡才能》（Humanity’s Last Prowess）。我对 AGI 转变中什么最重要的总框架。

本人所写，《一份清单中的未来》（The Future in One List）。一份关于将定义未来十年的技术的更广阔地图。
Related to the essay
与本文相关
Leopold Aschenbrenner, Situational Awareness, 2024. An AGI Manifesto.
利奥波德·阿申布伦纳（Leopold Aschenbrenner），《态势感知》（Situational Awareness），2024 年。一部 AGI 宣言。
AI 2027, 2025. A scenario forecast for AI over the coming years.
《AI 2027》，2025 年。关于未来几年 AI 的情景预测。

萨姆·奥特曼（Sam Altman），《融合》（The Merge），2017 年。关于人类与机器认知将汇合的早期论证之一。

里奇·萨顿，《苦涩的教训》。两页纸。在本文中被多次引用。
Onboarding to AI alignment
AI 对齐入门
Best single starting point: Robert Miles, Intro to AI Safety, Remastered. The clearest one-video primer on why this matters at all.
最佳单一起点：罗伯特·迈尔斯（Robert Miles），《AI 安全导论（重制版）》（Intro to AI Safety, Remastered）。关于为什么这件事从根本上重要的最清晰的单视频入门。
For more depth, Rob Miles’s full AI safety playlist, the AI Safety Map of the field’s organizations and research agendas, and aisafety.info for a curated Q&A-style guide.
想要更深入，可看 Rob Miles 的完整 AI 安全播放列表、该领域组织与研究议程的 AI Safety Map，以及以精选问答形式呈现的 aisafety.info。
AI scaling laws
AI 规模定律
Kaplan et al., Scaling Laws for Neural Language Models, OpenAI, 2020. The paper that turned “more data and compute keep helping” from an intuition into a power-law. The empirical foundation under the whole Bitter Lesson argument.
Kaplan 等人，《神经语言模型的规模定律》（Scaling Laws for Neural Language Models），OpenAI，2020 年。这篇论文把“更多数据和算力会持续有帮助”从一种直觉变成了一条幂律。它是整个“苦涩的教训”论证之下的经验基础。
Hoffmann et al., Training Compute-Optimal Large Language Models (Chinchilla), DeepMind, 2022. Corrected the data-to-parameters ratio modern frontier models train at.
Hoffmann 等人，《以计算最优方式训练大语言模型》（Training Compute-Optimal Large Language Models，即 Chinchilla），DeepMind，2022 年。修正了现代前沿模型训练时所采用的数据与参数比率。
Core BCI evidence
核心 BCI 证据
Tang et al., Semantic reconstruction of continuous language from non-invasive brain recordings, Nature Neuroscience, 2023.
Tang 等人，《从非侵入式脑记录中对连续语言进行语义重建》，Nature Neuroscience，2023 年。
Willett et al., High-performance brain-to-text communication via handwriting, Nature, 2021.
Willett 等人，《通过手写实现高性能脑到文本通信》，Nature，2021 年。
Metzger et al., A high-performance neuroprosthesis for speech decoding, Nature, 2023.
Metzger 等人，《一种用于语音解码的高性能神经假体》，Nature，2023 年。
Scotti et al., MindEye: fMRI-to-image with contrastive learning, 2023.
Scotti 等人，《MindEye：用对比学习实现 fMRI 到图像》，2023 年。
Foundation models for the brain
大脑的基础模型
Caro et al., BrainLM: A foundation model for brain activity recordings.
Caro 等人，《BrainLM：一种用于脑活动记录的基础模型》。
Banville, d’Ascoli, Dahan, Rapin et al., Meta AI, NeuralBench: A Unifying Framework to Benchmark NeuroAI Models, 2026.
Banville、d’Ascoli、Dahan、Rapin 等人，Meta AI，《NeuralBench：一个统一的 NeuroAI 模型基准框架》，2026 年。
NeuroAI and alignment
神经 AI 与对齐
Trent McConaghy, bci/acc: A Pragmatic Path to Compete with Artificial Superintelligence. The case for BCI as an alignment lever, from inside the field.
Trent McConaghy，《bci/acc：一条与人工超级智能竞争的务实路径》。从领域内部出发，论证 BCI 作为一根对齐杠杆。
Patrick Mineault and Joanne Peng, NeuroAI for AI Safety, Amaranth Foundation. A rigorous roadmap for neuro AI safety.
Patrick Mineault 与 Joanne Peng，《面向 AI 安全的神经 AI》（NeuroAI for AI Safety），Amaranth Foundation。一份严谨的神经 AI 安全路线图。
Community resources
社区资源
The community-curated awesome-brain-decoding repository.
由社区整理的 awesome-brain-decoding 代码仓库。
Brain-Computer Interfaces: Exploring the Last Frontier, Martin Krag, byFounders. A thoughtful investment thesis presenting a closely aligned vision.
Martin Krag，《脑机接口：探索最后的前沿》（Brain-Computer Interfaces: Exploring the Last Frontier），byFounders。一份深思熟虑的投资论点，呈现了一种高度相近的愿景。
Adjacent: brain emulation and connectomics
相邻领域：脑仿真与连接组学
Anders Sandberg and Nick Bostrom, Whole Brain Emulation: A Roadmap, Future of Humanity Institute, 2008. The canonical reference for what WBE would actually require, from substrate scanning through compute.
Anders Sandberg 与 Nick Bostrom，《全脑仿真：一份路线图》（Whole Brain Emulation: A Roadmap），未来人类研究所（Future of Humanity Institute），2008 年。关于 WBE 实际需要什么——从基质扫描到计算——的经典参考文献。
Isaak Freeman, WBE feasibility thesis. Recent cost analysis putting the all-in figure for human emulation at roughly $5-50 billion.
Isaak Freeman，WBE 可行性论文。近期的成本分析认为，人类仿真的全部成本约为 50 亿到 500 亿美元。
Appendix D: The Modality Map
附录 D：模态图谱
A reference breakdown of the modalities named throughout this manifesto. The interactive comparison is in the modality map; below is each modality in detail:
对本宣言中提及的各种模态的参考拆解。交互式对照见模态图谱；以下是每种模态的详细说明：
The Full Modality Map
完整模态图谱
Non-invasive BCIs
非侵入式 BCI
EEG: Measures electrical activity at the scalp with millisecond precision. Cheap and scalable but spatially noisy, it is the main test bed for early brain foundation models like AllJoined’s.
EEG：以毫秒级精度测量头皮处的电活动。便宜且可规模化，但空间上嘈杂，是 AllJoined 等早期大脑基础模型的主要试验场。
fNIRS: Uses near-infrared light to track blood oxygenation, an indirect signal that lags neural activity by several seconds. It is wearable, and Kernel is one of its leading commercial platforms.
fNIRS：用近红外光追踪血氧，这是一种滞后于神经活动数秒的间接信号。它是可穿戴的，Kernel 是其领先的商业平台之一。
Ultrasound: Uses sound waves to image blood flow and stimulate targeted regions deep in the brain. Still early, but promising for compact read-and-write interfaces. Butterfly Network put ultrasound on a chip; Nudge and Merge Labs are applying it to the brain.
超声：用声波对血流成像，并刺激大脑深处的目标区域。仍处于早期，但对紧凑的读写接口很有前景。Butterfly Network 把超声做进了芯片；Nudge 和 Merge Labs 正在把它应用于大脑。
MEG: Measures magnetic fields produced by neural activity with millisecond precision. Once limited to room- sized cryogenic scanners, it is becoming wearable through OPMs.
MEG：以毫秒级精度测量神经活动产生的磁场。一度仅限于房间大小的低温扫描仪，如今正通过 OPM 变得可穿戴。
fMRI: Uses powerful magnetic fields to track changes in blood oxygenation, an indirect measure of neural activity. It offers millimeter-scale spatial resolution and has enabled some of the best non-invasive semantic reconstructions, but it is slow, expensive, and confined to room-sized scanners.
fMRI：用强大的磁场追踪血氧变化，这是神经活动的一种间接测量。它提供毫米级空间分辨率，并使一些最好的非侵入式语义重建成为可能，但它缓慢、昂贵，并被限制在房间大小的扫描仪中。
Invasive BCIs
侵入式 BCI
Endovascular: Guides an electrode through the jugular vein into a blood vessel beside the motor cortex. Synchron’s Stentrode avoids open-brain surgery, trading signal precision for a far lower surgical burden.
血管内：通过颈静脉把电极引导到运动皮层旁的血管中。Synchron 的 Stentrode 避免了开颅手术，以信号精度换取低得多的手术负担。
ECoG: Places an electrode grid directly on the brain’s surface, capturing cleaner signals than EEG without penetrating the cortex. It has enabled speech decoding at 78 words per minute.
ECoG（皮层脑电图）：把电极网格直接放在大脑表面，在不穿透皮层的情况下捕捉比 EEG 更干净的信号。它已经实现了每分钟 78 词的语音解码。
sEEG: Inserts thin depth electrodes into cortical and subcortical regions. Widely used to localize seizures, it has produced large clinical archives of human intracranial recordings.
sEEG（立体定向脑电图）：把纤细的深部电极插入皮层和皮层下区域。被广泛用于定位癫痫发作，并产生了大量人类颅内记录的临床档案。
Intracortical: Inserts microelectrodes into the cortex to record individual neurons, providing the highest- fidelity signals. Used by Neuralink and Utah arrays, it enabled Stanford’s 90-character-per-minute handwriting decoder but surgery and long-term durability remain major barriers to scale.
皮层内：把微电极插入皮层以记录单个神经元，提供最高保真的信号。被 Neuralink 和犹他阵列（Utah arrays）使用，它使斯坦福每分钟 90 个字符的手写解码器成为可能，但手术和长期耐久性仍然是规模化的主要障碍。
The Future
未来
NV-Center: Defects in diamonds that detect tiny magnetic fields at room temperature. Not yet practical for BCI, but huge potential if we make it work.
NV 色心：钻石中的缺陷，可在室温下检测微小磁场。对 BCI 尚不实用，但如果我们能让它奏效，潜力巨大。
Appendix E: Glossary
附录 E：词汇表
ALS: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis. Progressive motor-neuron disease; one of the conditions BCI work has historically focused on.
肌萎缩侧索硬化症（渐冻症）。进行性运动神经元疾病；历史上 BCI 工作所聚焦的病症之一。
Bandwidth: How much information you can send and receive per unit of time.
带宽：单位时间内可以发送和接收多少信息。
Bandwidth gap: The vast mismatch between how fast machines can think and communicate, and how slowly humans can process information.
带宽差距：机器思考和通信的速度，与人类处理信息的缓慢速度之间的巨大错配。
BIDS: Brain Imaging Data Structure. A community standard for organizing neuroimaging data (MRI, EEG, MEG) in a uniform directory layout. Adoption is still uneven.
脑成像数据结构。一种以统一目录布局组织神经影像数据（MRI、EEG、MEG）的社区标准。采用程度仍不均衡。
Connectome: The full wiring diagram of a nervous system: every neuron and every connection between them.
连接组：一个神经系统的完整接线图谱，包括每一个神经元以及它们之间的每一条连接。
Diffusion models: Image-generation models that learn to reverse a noise process: start with pure noise, denoise step by step into a coherent image.
扩散模型：学习反转噪声过程的图像生成模型：从纯噪声开始，逐步去噪，直至形成一幅连贯的图像。
Dura mater: The tough outer membrane enveloping the brain and spinal cord. Semi-invasive devices sit beneath the skull but rest on top of this layer.
硬脑膜：包裹大脑和脊髓的坚韧外膜。半侵入式设备位于颅骨之下，但搁在这一层之上。
EEG: Electroencephalography. Reads voltages off the scalp. Cheap, ubiquitous, noisy.
脑电图。从头皮读取电压。便宜、普遍、嘈杂。
Endovascular: Electrodes delivered through blood vessels rather than open craniotomy.
血管内：通过血管而非开颅手术递送电极。
Exocortex: An external digital layer of your mind that extends your memory, attention, and mental computation beyond your biological brain while functioning as part of it.
外皮层：你心智的一个外部数字层，把记忆、注意力和心智运算延伸到生物大脑之外，同时作为大脑的一部分运作。
FAIR: Meta’s Fundamental AI Research lab. The open-research arm of Meta AI.
Meta 的基础人工智能研究实验室。Meta AI 的开放研究臂膀。
fMRI: Functional magnetic resonance imaging. Room-sized scanner with millimeter spatial resolution; the highest-quality non-invasive signal we have, at the lowest portability.
功能性磁共振成像。房间大小的扫描仪，具有毫米级空间分辨率；是我们拥有的最高质量的非侵入式信号，便携性也最低。
fNIRS: Functional near-infrared spectroscopy. Measures blood oxygenation through the skull using infrared. Cheap, wearable, slow.
功能性近红外光谱。用红外线透过颅骨测量血氧。便宜、可穿戴、缓慢。
Glial signaling: Communication between glial cells, the non-neuronal majority of brain cells. Increasingly suspected to modulate cognition, not just support neurons.
胶质细胞信号传导：胶质细胞（脑细胞中非神经元的多数）之间的通信。越来越被怀疑不仅是支持神经元，还能调节认知。
Hemodynamic: Relating to blood flow. Sensors such as fNIRS read the blood-oxygenation shadow of neural activity through the skull.
血流动力学：与血流相关。fNIRS 等传感器透过颅骨读取神经活动的血氧“影子”。
Interpretability: The effort to understand what happens inside a neural network: which internal features and circuits produce its behavior.
可解释性：努力理解神经网络内部发生了什么：哪些内部特征和回路产生了它的行为。
Limbic system: A set of deep brain structures that drive emotion, motivation, and reward. It is the circuitry that advertising has always tried to reach from the outside.
边缘系统：驱动情绪、动机和奖励的一组深部脑结构。它是广告一直试图从外部触及的回路。
MEG: Magnetoencephalography. It reads the tiny magnetic fields cast by neural activity. Millisecond timing, but classically needs a room-sized cryogenic scanner; OPMs are a new wearable path.
脑磁图。它读取神经活动投下的微小磁场。具有毫秒级时间精度，但传统上需要房间大小的低温扫描仪；OPM 是一条新的可穿戴路径。
Microelectrode arrays: Tiny silicon chips with dozens to hundreds of hair-thin needles, surgically inserted into brain tissue to record the firing of individual neurons.
微电极阵列：带有数十到数百根发丝般细针的微型硅芯片，通过手术插入脑组织以记录单个神经元的放电。
Microtubules: Tube-shaped protein structures inside neurons. Some theories (Penrose-Hameroff) propose they support quantum computation; mainstream neuroscience is still skeptical.
微管：神经元内部的管状蛋白质结构。一些理论（彭罗斯—哈梅罗夫，Penrose-Hameroff）提出它们支持量子计算；主流神经科学对此仍持怀疑态度。
Motor cortex: The strip of cortex that plans and executes voluntary movement. Easiest brain region to decode from, because intentions there map cleanly to body actions.
运动皮层：计划和执行随意运动的那条皮层。最容易从中解码的脑区，因为那里的意图干净地映射到身体动作。
NWB: Neurodata Without Borders. A standardized data format and ecosystem for neurophysiology recordings. The closest thing to a common schema across labs.
无边界神经数据。一种用于神经生理学记录的标准化数据格式和生态系统。是跨实验室最接近通用模式的东西。
OPM: Optically-pumped magnetometer. A chip-scale magnetic-field sensor that works at room temperature; the path to wearable MEG.
光泵磁力计。一种在室温下工作的芯片级磁场传感器；通往可穿戴 MEG 的路径。
Phase Lock Window: The brief interval between recognizing that AI will surpass us and the moment it does; the span in which humanity still has the agency to shape the terms of the relationship. Roughly now to the mid-2030s.
相位锁定窗口：从认识到 AI 将超越我们，到它真正超越我们的那一刻之间的短暂间隔；人类仍有能动性去塑造这种关系条款的时段。大致从现在到 2030 年代中期。
Quantum effects: Hypothesis that quantum-mechanical phenomena (coherence, entanglement) play a functional role in brain computation. Live but speculative, no consensus evidence.
量子效应：一种假说，认为量子力学现象（相干、纠缠）在脑计算中发挥功能作用。仍然活跃但属于推测，没有共识性证据。
Ransomware: Malicious software that encrypts a victim’s data or device and demands payment to restore access.
勒索软件：加密受害者的数据或设备、并要求付款以恢复访问的恶意软件。
RLHF: Reinforcement learning from human feedback. The training step where humans rank a model’s outputs to steer it toward preferred behavior.
基于人类反馈的强化学习。人类对模型输出进行排序、以将其引导向偏好行为的训练步骤。
sEMG: Surface electromyography. Reads electrical activity of muscles through skin electrodes. Well-suited to subtle motor intent and silent speech.
表面肌电图。通过皮肤电极读取肌肉的电活动。非常适合细微的运动意图和无声言语。
Signal-to-noise: How strong the wanted signal is against background noise; the core obstacle to reading the brain through skull and skin.
信噪比：有用信号相对于背景噪声有多强；透过颅骨和皮肤读取大脑的核心障碍。
Whole Brain Emulation: A hypothetical process of scanning, mapping, and simulating a biological brain on a computer so that the digital model functions identically to the original mind.
全脑仿真：在计算机上扫描、测绘并模拟一个生物大脑，使数字模型与原初心智功能相同的一种假想过程。
wpm: Words per minute. A standard measure of communication speed.
每分钟词数。通信速度的标准度量。
